TL;DR: 本文研究了LLM生成的基数偏好判断是否具有自一致性（即能否用单一效用函数来表征），通过统计检验和可解释度量发现，六种LLM在航班、公寓和酒店等场景中产生的偏好判断存在显著且持续的不一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于LLM的偏好估计管线假设LLM给出的数值偏好判断（如愿意支付金额）可被单一效用函数近似复现，但这一前提是否成立从未被严格验证。若不成立，则下游基于效用函数的决策将不可靠。

Method: 提出了统计检验方法和可解释度量，用于量化观测到的LLM偏好响应与最佳拟合的自一致效用函数之间的偏离程度。实验覆盖航班、公寓、酒店三类示例，在六个不同的LLM上进行了系统评估。

Result: 实验结果表明，所有六种LLM生成的基数偏好判断均表现出显著且持续的不一致性，即LLM的偏好判断无法被单一效用函数忠实概括。

Conclusion: LLM派生的偏好判断不能简单地用单一效用函数来拟合，这挑战了当前基于LLM偏好估计算法的核心假设，提示研究者需要重新审视该管线或开发更灵活的偏好表征方法。

Abstract: Agents increasingly interpret a person's natural-language preferences by querying an LLM for numerical preference judgments, e.g., by asking how much the person would be willing to pay for an item. A growing body of work estimates a utility function from these judgments and then chooses actions based on their estimated utility. This pipeline assumes the judgments are approximately self-consistent: that a single utility function can reproduce them. But are they? To study this question, we measure the self-consistency of cardinal LLM preference judgments. For example, the difference in stated willingness-to-pay between two items should match the stated payment that makes a person indifferent to exchanging them. We develop statistical tests and interpretable measures of how far observed responses depart from the best-fitting self-consistent utility function. Experiments with flight, apartment, and hotel examples across six LLMs reveal large persistent inconsistencies. This suggests that LLM-derived preference judgments cannot be faithfully summarized by a single utility function.

</details>


### [134] [GraphWake: Group Polarization via Memory-Mediated Polarization Cascade in LLM-Agent Communities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17665)
*Haoran Bu,Zejian Chen,Litian Zhang,Xi Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的安全威胁——记忆介导的极化级联（Memory-Mediated Polarization Cascade），利用LLM驱动的智能体记忆系统作为持久化通道、公开讨论作为传播通道，通过三个阶段的攻击流程（暴露与记忆保留、检索与再现、迭代传播）诱导群体极化，并在GraphWake框架中实例化了该威胁，实验证明其能显著加剧群体极化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的操纵LLM智能体以诱导群体极化的方法（如操纵提示词、构建回音室）在实际场景中难以实现，需要一个更隐蔽、更易实施的攻击方式。本文旨在填补这一空白，提出一种利用智能体记忆机制的新型攻击威胁。

Method: 提出Memory-Mediated Polarization Cascade威胁模型，包含三个阶段：(1)暴露与记忆保留：攻击者向少量目标智能体注入支持其立场的论点；(2)检索与再现：通过立场中立的话题提示目标智能体检索并再现所保留的论点；(3)迭代传播：未受处理的智能体受再现论点影响后继续传播。在GraphWake框架中实现三个组件：立场支持论证知识图谱构建知识化论点、公理导向三元组选择进行可靠筛选、立场中立记忆提示触发并发检索与再现。

Result: 在多个讨论场景和记忆系统上的实验表明，GraphWake能够显著增加群体极化程度，验证了该威胁模型的可行性和有效性。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了一种社区层面的极化风险，提醒研究者LLM智能体社交平台的记忆机制可能被攻击者利用作为传播极化的通道，需要加强防御措施。

Abstract: LLM-driven agents can autonomously exchange opinions on online platforms and form communities. Such agent-operated social platforms raise a new security concern: attackers may manipulate agents to induce group polarization. Existing methods manipulate agent prompts or construct echo chambers, both of which are difficult to realize in practice. We therefore formulate a new threat, Memory-Mediated Polarization Cascade, which uses agent memory as a persistence channel and public discussion as a propagation channel. This threat contains three stages. During exposure and memory retention, the attacker exposes a small set of target agents to arguments that reinforce their respective stated stances. The targets' memory systems then process and retain these arguments. During retrieval and reproduction, a shared stance-neutral discussion cues the targets to retrieve and reproduce their respective retained arguments. During iterative propagation, untreated agents influenced by the reproduced arguments restate and spread them. We instantiate this threat in GraphWake with three components: (i) stance-support argumentation knowledge graphs construct knowledge-based arguments; (ii) axiom-oriented triple selection distills them for reliable retention and reproduction; and (iii) stance-neutral memory cueing triggers concurrent retrieval and reproduction, initiating propagation. Experiments across multiple discussions and memory systems show that GraphWake substantially increases group polarization. These findings reveal a community-level polarization risk.

</details>


### [135] [Auditing Self-Evolution in Financial Agents: Capability Gains, Security Drift, and Execution-Interface Mismatch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17684)
*Jialong Li,Jialing Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文审计了三种自我进化的智能体（SkillOpt、Agent Workflow Memory、ReasoningBank）在模拟电子银行场景中的安全性，发现仅看准确率会掩盖严重的攻击面扩大与未授权状态改变等安全风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自我进化智能体虽能提升能力，但传统评估只看进化后的准确率，无法反映其是否破坏原有正确行为或引入安全漏洞，尤其在金融场景中安全风险不容忽视。

Method: 采用匹配的良性获取轨迹、密封评估端点、执行基准确认和独立状态回放等方法，在Qwen 3.7 Flash上对三种智能体进行安全审计，追踪能力、暴露度、攻击成功率及未授权金融状态变化，并进行后验敏感性测试。

Result: SkillOpt使良性效用从0.741升至0.837，但暴露度从0.820升至0.943，整体攻击成功率从0.496升至0.530，未授权金融状态变化达0.685。ReasoningBank提升效用至0.859且未增加整体ASR。AWM则暴露了评估环境的兼容性问题——移除WebArena文本动作信封后效用从0.319升至0.756，但暴露度和ASR大幅上升。

Conclusion: 审计自我进化金融智能体必须同时追踪能力回归、攻击面接触、未授权金融状态变化及工件与执行器的兼容性，而不能仅依赖准确率指标。

Abstract: Self-evolving agents turn experience into reusable skills, workflows, or memories, but post-evolution accuracy alone does not show whether learned behavior preserves previously correct behavior or security. We audit SkillOpt, Agent Workflow Memory (AWM), and ReasoningBank in simulated e-banking using matched benign acquisition trajectories, sealed evaluation endpoints, execution-grounded checks, and independent state replay. On Qwen 3.7 Flash, SkillOpt raises benign utility from 0.741 to 0.837 while exposure to injected content rises from 0.820 to 0.943. Conditional attack success after exposure falls from 0.605 to 0.562, yet overall attack success rate (ASR) rises from 0.496 to 0.530 and unauthorized financial state changes rise to 0.685. Across three independently evolved lineages, capability, exposure, and unauthorized-state changes increase in all three, whereas ASR increases in only two. ReasoningBank raises utility to 0.859 without increasing aggregate ASR, although unauthorized state changes remain slightly above Static. AWM reveals a separate evaluation hazard: a literal WebArena text-action envelope disrupts tool execution in our native function-calling executor. In a post-hoc sensitivity test, removing only that envelope restores utility from 0.319 to 0.756, while exposure rises from 0.299 to 0.909 and ASR from 0.195 to 0.575. Auditing self-evolving financial agents therefore requires tracking regressions, attack-surface contact, unauthorized financial-state change, and artifact-executor compatibility, not accuracy alone.

</details>


### [136] [Mixture-of-Expert Blocks Contain Strong Hallucination Detection Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17687)
*Joao Fonseca,Rodrigo Rodrigues,Paolo Romano*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出InnerExpert，首个利用MoE（混合专家）架构内部信号（如路由器熵、专家分歧度、专家使用模式）进行逐token幻觉检测的方法，无需额外前向传播，在五个数据集和两种MoE架构上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型普遍存在幻觉问题，现有检测方法多集中在句子或答案级别，缺乏逐token级别的检测能力，无法精确定位幻觉片段。MoE架构中丰富的内部信号（路由器熵、专家分歧度等）尚未被用于幻觉检测，填补这一空白是本文的动机。

Method: InnerExpert方法将MoE特有的路由级信号（路由器熵、专家分歧度、专家使用模式）与标准Transformer信号相结合，构成紧凑的逐token特征向量；采用轻量级分类器进行检测，训练标签由LLM-as-a-judge流水线自动生成，无需人工标注，支持模型的持续更新。

Result: 在五个数据集和两种MoE架构上，InnerExpert均优于现有方法，达到最高0.91的答案级AUROC和0.76的token级AUROC，且仅需单次前向传播即可完成检测。

Conclusion: MoE架构内部信号对逐token幻觉检测具有显著价值，是密度架构无法提供的独特优势。InnerExpert验证了这一方向的可行性，并为无需人工标注的持续模型更新提供了实用路径。

Abstract: Despite their widespread use, Large Language Models (LLMs) remain limited by a fundamental problem: the generation of plausible but false content, known as hallucinations. Most existing detection methods operate at the answer or sentence level, yet per-token detection is essential for localizing hallucinated spans and enabling fine-grained interventions. In this paper, we explore the use of the Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) paradigm to address this gap. In MoE architectures, a single forward pass activates a sparse subset of experts (i.e., distinct feedforward networks per layer) via a routing mechanism, producing internal signals (e.g., router entropy, expert disagreement, and expert usage patterns) that are unavailable in dense architectures and have not been previously exploited for hallucination detection. To this end, we introduce InnerExpert, the first method to leverage these MoE-specific signals for per-token hallucination detection. InnerExpert combines routing-level and standard transformer signals into compact per-token feature vectors, classified by a lightweight detector trained on labels produced by an LLM-as-a-judge pipeline, which enables continuous model updates without manual annotation. Our results show that InnerExpert outperforms existing methods across five datasets and two MoE architectures, achieving up to 0.91 answer-level and 0.76 token-level AUROC, while requiring only a single forward pass.

</details>


### [137] [Accuracy and Robustness of Model Cascades Under Data Perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17711)
*Pallavi Mitra,Jai Kushwaha,Felix Biessmann*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了置信度驱动的模型级联在图像分类中面对输入退化（静态损坏和序列扰动）时的路由行为，识别出三种失效模式，并强调节能级联需要超越仅考虑干净准确率的评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测级联虽能显著降低AI模型能耗，但其有效性依赖于置信度路由的可靠性。输入退化可能改变模型置信度和路由决策，而现有评估主要关注干净数据上的表现，忽视了分布偏移下路由的可靠性问题。

Method: 选取位于准确率、路由质量和能耗帕累托最优点的模型级联（可实现最多10倍CO2减排），在静态损坏和序列扰动下研究级联的路由决策变化，分析输入分布偏移时路由行为的改变。

Result: 识别出三种失效模式：静态损坏要么（1）破坏路由信号而大模型仍有用，要么（2）使两个模型均退化导致延后无法恢复准确率；序列扰动揭示第三种模式：预测稳定但延后被抑制，产生稳定但不可靠的预测。

Conclusion: 节能模型级联的评估必须超越干净准确率，需显式关注分布偏移下的路由可靠性，以确保其实际部署中的鲁棒性和可信度。

Abstract: Prediction cascades significantly reduce energy consumption of Artificial Intelligence (AI) models while maintaining high predictive performance. The idea is that easy inputs are routed through a lightweight small model, and difficult uncertain cases are deferred to a larger model. While this design can improve computational efficiency on clean data, its effectiveness depends on the reliability of confidence-based routing. Input degradations, such as static corruptions and sequential perturbations, can shift model confidence and routing decisions. In this paper, we study confidence-based cascade frameworks for image classification and investigate how such degradations affect their confidence-based deferral behavior. We select a model cascade at the pareto-optimum of accuracy, routing quality, and energy consumption that achieves competitive predictive performance with an up to 10-fold decrease in CO$_2$ emissions. We study the behavior of that model cascade under input corruptions and analyze how the cascade's routing decisions change when the input distribution shifts. Our analysis identifies three failure modes. Static corruptions either (1) break the routing signal while the large model remains useful, or (2) degrade both models so deferral no longer recovers accuracy. Sequential perturbations reveal a third mode: predictions stabilize but deferral suppresses, yielding stable but unreliable predictions. These findings demonstrate that energy efficient model cascades require evaluation beyond clean accuracy, with explicit attention to routing reliability under distribution shift.

</details>


### [138] [Beyond Suspicious Steps: Ontological Trust in Long-Horizon Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17718)
*An He,Yao Wang,Haibin Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"本体论信任"（ontological trust）概念，并实现为RGE在线监控器，从角色（Role）、目标（Goal）、证据（Evidence）三个维度分解任务条件化的轨迹前缀信任度。RGE仅用LLM提取结构化任务和步骤表征，信任状态更新、投影和干预决策均为确定性计算，输出可重放、可审计的信任轨迹。在OSWorld、FinanceBench和EICU-AC三个跨域基准上，RGE在前缀漂移检测上优于基于规则、判官和盾牌的基线，最大两个估计器模型在所有基准上Drift F1超过93%，良性覆盖率不低于95.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视野智能体在多个步骤、工具和观察中运行，现有监控器主要检查局部合规、给出最终轨迹判定或进行通用风险评分，无法直接估计"轨迹前缀是否仍对应于用户授权的任务"这一关系。漂移可能静默积累——每一步工具调用看似合理，但前缀正滑向更宽泛的角色、相邻目标或用户从未提供的证据，因此需要一种前缀级别的任务条件化信任评估方法。

Method: RGE将本体论信任沿三个维度分解：角色（agent是否越出授权范围）、目标（行为是否偏离用户指定目标）、证据（是否使用未经用户授权或超出观察范围的证据）。RGE仅利用LLM推导结构化的任务与步骤表征，随后的信任状态更新、维度投影以及干预决策全部采用确定性计算，从而生成可重放、可审计的信任轨迹而非单一的整体判决。作者构建了跨域轨迹语料库，涵盖良性执行、成对前缀漂移和伪一致性失败三类场景。

Result: 在跨域语料库上，RGE在前缀配对漂移检测上优于适应的规则式、判官式和盾牌式基线。使用两个较大的估计器模型时，RGE在每个基准上的Drift F1超过93%，同时将良性覆盖率保持在95.8%以上。伪一致性检测更具挑战性：其检测效果取决于任务完成是否对外部可见，作者将其表征为该方法的结构性局限。

Conclusion: RGE通过将信任分解为角色、目标和证据三个确定性的可审计维度，提供了一种比端到端评判器更可追溯、可回放的前缀级智能体信任监控方案。实验表明其在漂移检测上表现优越，但伪一致性失败的检测存在结构性边界——当任务完成状态无法从外部观察时，其效力受限。

Abstract: Long-horizon agents increasingly operate across many steps, tools, and observa- tions. In this setting, the relevant oversight question is not only whether each action is locally valid, but whether the evolving trajectory still corresponds to the task the user authorized. Drift can accumulate quietly: an agent may call the right tool with plausible arguments at every step, while its prefix moves toward a broader role, an adjacent objective, or evidence the user never supplied. Existing monitors mostly check local compliance, deliver final-trace verdicts, or score generic risk; they do not directly estimate this prefix-level relation. We introduce ontological trust, a task-conditioned property of trajectory prefixes, and instantiate it as RGE, an online monitor that decomposes trust along Role, Goal, and Evidence. RGE uses LLMs only to derive structured task and step representations; trust-state updates, projec- tions, and intervention decisions are deterministic, so the output is a replayable and auditable trust trajectory rather than a single end-to-end judge verdict. We construct a cross-domain trajectory corpus from OSWorld, FinanceBench, and EICU-AC, covering benign executions, prefix-paired drift, and pseudo-consistency failures. On this corpus, RGE outperforms adapted rule-, judge-, and shield-style baselines on prefix-paired drift detection. With the two larger estimator models, it exceeds 93% Drift F1 on every benchmark while keeping benign coverage at or above 95.8%. Pseudo-consistency is harder: detection depends on whether task completion is externally visible, a structural limit we characterize empirically.

</details>


### [139] [Evaluating the Diversity of AI-Generated Content with Diversity Profiles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17731)
*Xiuyuan Hu,Xuege Hou,Guoqing Liu,Yang Zhao,Jieran Li,Dongbiao Sun,José Miguel Hernández-Lobato,Hao Zhang,Xue Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出现有生成式AI多样性评估方法将多样性压缩为单一标量分数存在根本性缺陷，提出"多样性轮廓"（diversity profiles）这一曲线值、条件感知的评估框架，通过参数化多样性族在不同阈值、尺度、指数或阶数下的连续评估，揭示比较结果在不同分辨率下是否稳健，从而提供更透明、分辨率感知的多样性评估方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多样性是评估生成式AI系统的核心标准，但现有的标量式多样性指标存在两个关键问题：一是不同指标编码了不同的归纳偏置，可能对同一组样本给出矛盾的评价排序；二是通过公理化分析和实证分析发现，没有任何代表性标量指标能同时满足所有理想性质，且高维嵌入空间中距离分布存在集中化和模态依赖问题。多样性评估被压缩为单一数字本质上是欠规范的（under-specified）。

Method: 本文采用三步骤方法：（1）综述代表性多样性指标；（2）从公理化角度分析各指标是否满足理想性质，从实证角度揭示高维空间中距离分布的集中化现象；（3）提出"多样性轮廓"框架：在指定表示和距离/核函数下，对参数化多样性族（涵盖阈值、尺度、指数或阶数等参数）进行全范围连续评估，生成曲线值摘要而非单一标量，并针对多个代表性指标族进行了具体实例化。

Result: 公理化分析表明没有代表性标量指标能同时满足所有期望性质；实证分析揭示高维表示空间可导致集中化、模态依赖的距离分布。多样性轮廓方法能够揭示比较结果是否在不同分辨率下保持稳健，还是受制于任意参数选择，并在多个代表性指标族上完成了实用验证。

Conclusion: 多样性轮廓为生成式AI内容的多样性评估提供了一个更透明、分辨率感知的替代框架，能够克服标量指标的固有局限，使评估结果对参数选择更具鲁棒性，有助于更可靠地比较不同生成系统的多样性表现。

Abstract: Diversity is a fundamental criterion for evaluating generative artificial intelligence (AI) systems, yet its measurement remains inherently ambiguous. Existing approaches typically represent generated samples in an embedding space, compute pairwise distances or similarities, and aggregate them into a single scalar score. Such scalar summaries are convenient, but they often encode different inductive biases and may yield contradictory rankings of the same sample sets. In this paper, we argue that diversity evaluation for AI-generated content is intrinsically under-specified when reduced to a single number. We first review representative diversity metrics, and then diagnose their limitations from two complementary perspectives: an axiomatic analysis showing that no representative scalar metric satisfies all desirable properties simultaneously, and an empirical analysis showing that high-dimensional representation spaces can induce concentrated, modality-dependent distance distributions. To address these issues, we propose diversity profiles: curve-valued, condition-aware summaries that evaluate a parameterized diversity family across a range of thresholds, scales, exponents, or orders under a specified representation and distance or kernel function. Diversity profiles reveal whether a comparison is robust across resolutions or instead depends on an arbitrary parameter choice. We instantiate profiles for several representative metric families and demonstrate their practical use in generative AI evaluation. Overall, diversity profiles provide a more transparent and resolution-aware framework for comparing the diversity of AI-generated content.

</details>


### [140] [Neuro-symbolic learning over OWL 2 DL via consequence-based compilation to differentiable circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17741)
*Olga Mashkova,Asaad Mohammedsaleh,Fernando Zhapa-Camacho,Robert Hoehndorf*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Baobab将SROIQ本体（OWL 2 DL）编译为命题决策图（SDD），在部分ABox监督下训练感知网络识别图像，并首次系统地刻画和缓解了非Horn描述逻辑中的推理捷径问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的神经符号（NeSy）学习方法要么将本体嵌入连续空间而放弃经典蕴涵，要么限于Horn片段EL++（只有单一典范模型），无法处理完整SROIQ表达能力下的推理与学习任务。

Method: 将SROIQ本体连同有限ABox编译为SDD（句法决策图）：先对命题核心进行基于后果的饱和计算，再在活跃域上实例化剩余的SROIQ特性（名义、数量限制、角色公理）；利用SDD的基于证据条件的加权模型计数来训练感知网络。

Result: 在涵盖所有SROIQ特性的本体上，CNN能学会读取由后继关系耦合的MNIST数字，并恢复独立感知无法发现的潜在本体概念；通过以查询的justifications为索引的混合分布，能够表示独立感知无法达到的校准后验，在真实图像MNIST任务上实现了贝叶斯最优后验，超越了单WMC和BEARS-ensemble假设类。

Conclusion: Baobab是首个在非Horn描述逻辑中刻画并缓解推理捷径的方法；编译器与表示定理的正确性已在Lean 4中机器验证。

Abstract: OWL 2 DL ontologies, grounded in the description logic $\mathcal{SROIQ}$, express large knowledge bases in biomedicine and the Semantic Web. Neuro-symbolic (NeSy) learners over description logics either embed the ontology in a continuous space, abandoning classical entailment, or restrict to the Horn fragment $\mathcal{EL}^{++}$, which has a single canonical model. We present Baobab, which compiles a $\mathcal{SROIQ}$ ontology with a finite ABox into a Sentential Decision Diagram (SDD): it saturates a propositional core under a consequence-based calculus and instantiates the remaining $\mathcal{SROIQ}$ features (nominals, number restrictions, and the role axioms) over the active domain. The SDD's evidence-conditioned weighted model count then trains a perception network to recognize real images under partial ABox supervision: on an ontology that exercises every distinctive $\mathcal{SROIQ}$ feature, a CNN learns to read MNIST digits coupled by a successor relation and recovers latent ontology concepts that an independent perception leaves at chance. When the supervision admits several ontology-consistent completions, an independent perception collapses onto one, a reasoning shortcut: we show that a mixture indexed by the query's justifications can represent the calibrated posterior no independent perception can, and that seeding it from the circuit's enumerated completions attains the Bayes-optimal posterior on a real-image MNIST task where single-WMC and learned mixtures (the BEARS-ensemble hypothesis class) do not: to our knowledge the first to characterize and mitigate reasoning shortcuts in a non-Horn description logic. Soundness of the compiler and the representation result are machine-checked in Lean 4. Code is available at https://github.com/bio-ontology-research-group/baobab.

</details>


### [141] [The Curious Case of Exploding DecPOMDPs: Containing the Fire through Policy Counting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17749)
*Nazlı Nur Karabulut,tanya Braun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将计算焦点从"计数智能体"转移到"计数策略"，用于解决DecPOMDP在智能体数量上指数爆炸的问题，并提出了基于紧凑表示的策略计数动态规划算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DecPOMDP是建模多智能体不确定性决策的通用框架，但其复杂度随智能体数量呈指数增长。已有方法通过划分具有对称性的智能体群组来压缩模型，但策略空间仍然爆炸，无法真正解决智能体数量带来的棘手的计算难题。

Method: 本文提出"策略计数DecPOMDP"（policy-counted DecPOMDPs），将计数对象从智能体转为策略，使得模型复杂度和评估成本对智能体数量呈多项式依赖。并进一步提出使用紧凑表示的策略计数动态规划（policy-counted dynamic programming）来高效求解该类问题。

Result: 通过将计数从智能体转向策略，实现了对智能体数量的可处理性（tractability），并证明了基于紧凑表示的策略计数动态规划方法能够高效求解策略计数DecPOMDP。

Conclusion: 本文重新定义了DecPOMDP中计数的作用对象（从智能体到策略），为多智能体规划在智能体数量维度上提供了新的可解途径。

Abstract: Decentralised partially observable Markov decision processes (DecPOMDPs) provide a general framework for modelling multi-agent decision making under uncertainty. However, DecPOMDPs are known to suffer from exponential complexity in the number of agents. One way to combat this intractability in agent numbers is to look at partitions of agents that exhibit a form of symmetry among agents, allowing for a compact encoding by counting. However, a challenge arises as the policy space explodes, even though the model complexity and evaluation cost reduce to a polynomial dependence. In this paper, we redirect our focus from counting agents to counting policies, which actually enables tractability in agent numbers for so called policy-counted DecPOMDPs. Further, we present policy-counted dynamic programming using the compact representation to solve policy-counted DecPOMDPs efficiently.

</details>


### [142] [D$^2$ACCI: A Dual-Loop Diagnostic Protocol for Evidence-Preserving Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17756)
*Xule Liu,Yijun Liu,Chao Li,Shao Kun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了D²ACCI协议，一种用于LLM智能体持久记忆系统的双循环诊断驱动迭代框架，配套DCR可观测性指标和D²ACCI-Eval可复用评估工具。实验在三个公开基准上取得优异成绩（LoCoMo 93.59%、LongMemEval 90.93%、PersonaMem-V2 57.20%），并通过成对消融实验证明部分模块带来统计显著增益，而BM25/RRF则作为受监控的功能开关保留。诊断审计显示富化trace在根因一致性上大幅优于仅结果标注。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体的持久记忆系统采用多阶段流水线（摄取、检索、过滤、生成），失败难以定位到具体阶段。现有评估仅报告聚合性能，缺乏成对统计比较、切片级非回归检验和阶段级诊断追踪，导致记忆系统迭代缺乏可靠的证据支撑。

Method: 提出D²ACCI（诊断驱动的、基于工件的闭环受控迭代）协议，包含外部诊断门控，基于成对证据、受保护切片监控和追踪级可定位性来推广、功能标记或拒绝记忆干预。配套提出DCR（分级可观测性指标）衡量失败是否保持可定位，以及D²ACCI-Eval可复用工件用于门控重放。在MemStack中实例化协议。

Result: 在LoCoMo（93.59%）、LongMemEval（90.93%）和PersonaMem-V2（57.20%）上取得成绩。五组成对消融实验表明：补充抽提、会话记忆检索和Forget Guard带来统计显著提升（+1.9到+3.7pp，p≤.003）；BM25/RRF被保留为受监控功能开关。诊断审计显示富化trace在根因一致性上显著优于仅结果标注；诊断工件DCR@3达98-100%，而仅结果日志为0%。

Conclusion: 稳健的记忆系统迭代需要可追踪、统计严谨、回归感知的证据，这正是D²ACCI填补的空白。与传统仅报告聚合性能的评估相比，该协议提供更精细的失败定位能力和更可信的干预决策依据。

Abstract: Memory is a key capability of LLM agents. Persistent memory extends this across sessions---enabling recall, revision, and personalization. Yet its multi-stage pipeline (ingestion, retrieval, filtering, generation) makes failures difficult to localize: end-to-end evaluation reveals that an error occurred, but not which stage caused it. Existing evaluations often report aggregate performance without paired statistical comparisons, slice-level non-regression checks, or stage-level diagnostic traces. We propose D$^2$ACCI (Diagnostic-Driven Artifact-based Closed-loop Controlled Iteration), a dual-loop protocol whose outer diagnostic gate promotes, feature-flags, or rejects memory interventions based on paired evidence, protected-slice monitoring, and trace-level localizability. We further introduce DCR, a graded observability metric that measures whether failures remain localizable, and D$^2$ACCI-Eval, a reusable artifact for gate replay. We instantiate the protocol in MemStack and evaluate on three public benchmarks, achieving 93.59% on LoCoMo, 90.93% on LongMemEval, and 57.20% on PersonaMem-V2. Five paired ablations show that supplement extraction, session-memory retrieval, and Forget Guard yield statistically significant gains (+1.9 to +3.7pp, all p $\le$ .003). In contrast, BM25/RRF is retained as a monitored feature flag---a distinction invisible to aggregate-only evaluation. A diagnostic audit shows enriched traces substantially improve root-cause agreement over result-only relabeling. Diagnostic artifacts reach 98--100% DCR@3 versus 0% for results-only logs. These results establish that robust memory-system iteration demands traceable, statistically grounded, and regression-aware evidence---exactly the gap D$^2$ACCI fills.

</details>


### [143] [StartupBench: Benchmarking General-Purpose Agents on Market-Validated End-to-End Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17800)
*Liya Zhu,Xin Ma,Tao Liu,Haodong Wang,Ge Zhang,Jingzhe Ding,Qingshui Gu,Yongjie Zhong,Jinxiang Meng,Yuan Gao,Yunqiu Zhou,Hao Zhu,Jifeng He,Yongzhi Liao,Xinyi Zhang,Chaoxin Li,Yi Zhu,Xi Lin,Duju Zeng,Xiang Gao,Wen Zhang,Yunyang Wang,Duo Wang,Huan Zhou,Zuo Wang,Jin Chen,Kaiyuan Zhang,Chuqian Yu,Tianhao Yu,Longxiang Liu,Jianbo Xue,Huimin Che,Jiahao Wang,Yujia Qin,Jiaheng Liu,Shen Yan,Xiaolong Chang,Wenhao Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出StartupBench，一个基于市场验证的AI初创公司产品构建的端到端智能体基准测试。与传统基于研究者预设任务的基准不同，StartupBench从实际被采用的产品中提取真实世界工作任务，通过细粒度评分标准进行评估。实验表明，即使最强的模型也仅能完成约30%的任务，揭示了当前通用智能体在复杂指令遵循和领域专业知识方面的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM和智能体基准大多依赖研究者自行选择的任务，无法准确反映AI系统在真实用户实际需求中的表现。作者希望构建一个以市场验证的AI产品为基础、反映真实工作流程的基准，从而更准确地衡量智能体在现实任务中的能力。

Method: 系统性地研究具有实际采用证明的AI初创公司产品，分析其产品工作流程和用户行为，从中提炼出跨多个专业领域的真实世界任务。将这些工作流程转化为完整的、以交付物为导向的任务，并设计细粒度评分标准来评估任务的复杂要求。在统一的智能体框架下对多个代表性模型进行评估。

Result: 即使最强的模型在StartupBench上也仅能成功完成约30%的任务，尽管在许多任务上取得了显著的局部进展。进一步分析发现，复杂指令遵循和领域专业知识是主要的失败来源。

Conclusion: 许多市场验证的真实工作流程仍超出当前通用智能体的可靠能力范围。StartupBench为衡量智能体端到端完成现实用户任务的能力提供了实证衡量标准，揭示了当前智能体在实际应用中的局限。

Abstract: Recent advances in Large Language Models(LLMs) and agents have substantially improved the ability of AI systems to execute complex tasks. Yet existing benchmarks largely rely on researcher-selected tasks, leaving uncertain whether such progress extends to the work that real-world users actually demand from AI systems. We introduce \textbf{StartupBench}, an E2E agent benchmark grounded in market-validated AI startup products. Rather than defining tasks from pre-defined assumptions about useful agent capabilities, we systematically study AI products with demonstrated adoption, together with their product workflows and users, to identify real-world tasks for which AI has established practical demand across diverse professional domains. We translate these workflows into complete deliverable-oriented tasks and evaluate them with fine-grained rubrics capturing their complex requirements. Across representative models evaluated under a unified agent harness, even the strongest model successfully completes only approximately 30\% of StartupBench, despite making substantial partial progress on many tasks. Further analysis identifies aspects like complex instruction following and domain-specific expertise as major sources of failure. Our results reveal that many market-validated workflows remain beyond the reliable capabilities of current general-purpose agents, establishing StartupBench as an empirical measure of progress toward E2E completions of real-world user tasks.

</details>


### [144] [ARASH: Adaptive Retrieval And Shot Selection for Tabular Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17856)
*Samirasadat Jamalidinan,Yue Xu,Kazem Cheshmi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ARASH方法，通过基于局部邻域分析的自适应、查询特定的样本选择策略，在不显著降低准确率的前提下，大幅减少TabPFN等表格基础模型的提示长度和内存使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格基础模型（如TabPFN）在表格预测任务上表现优异，但需要大量计算资源且频繁重训练不切实际。虽然上下文学习（尤其是少样本提示）提供了资源高效的替代方案，但如何识别最相关的样本行作为提示仍然是一个挑战。

Method: ARASH在训练集中通过局部邻域分析来选择合适的提示样本，实现自适应的、针对特定查询的检索与样本选择策略，以增强少样本提示的效果。

Result: ARASH将TabPFN的提示长度和内存使用分别减少了1261.5倍和2.56倍，同时保持了可比较的准确率。

Conclusion: ARASH证明了通过智能样本选择可以显著提升表格基础模型的效率，在保持准确性的同时大幅降低计算和内存开销，为表格预测任务提供了一种资源高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Tabular prediction is a critical task across numerous applications. The recent success of large language models has sparked various approaches for adapting them to the tabular domain. A prevalent strategy involves training or fine-tuning specialized Tabular Foundation Models (TFMs) such as TabPFN. However, TFMs require substantial computational resources, and frequent model retraining is often impractical. In-context learning (ICL), specifically, few-shot prompting, offers a resource-efficient alternative to enhance performance. Yet, identifying the most relevant rows to serve as shots remains a challenge for tabular data. This paper introduces ARASH (Adaptive, query-specific Retrieval And Shot selection), a method that improves TFM efficiency by selecting optimal shots based on local neighborhood analysis within the training set. Our results demonstrate that ARASH reduces the prompt length and memory usage of TabPFN by 1261.5$\times$ and 2.56$\times$, respectively, while providing comparable accuracy.

</details>


### [145] [AutoResearch: Insight In, Hallucination Out](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17906)
*Yiming Ren,Xiang Liu,Qumeng Sun,Xiao Zhang,Jiahao Li,Haoyang Zhang,Junjie Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AutoResearch，一个两阶段自主研究系统，将Idea Generation（想法生成）与Idea Execution（想法执行）连接起来，在跨模态检索、系统优化和基准驱动的机器学习等场景中实现从想法到实验再到结论的全流程科学化落地，有效减少幻觉和不可靠结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主研究系统虽能执行长研究流程，但自动化本身不能保证研究过程保持科学严谨性，存在想法不可靠、实验结果无法验证等问题，需要将科学性原则系统性地嵌入自主研究过程。

Method: AutoResearch采用两阶段架构：在Idea Generation阶段，持续整合新兴研究信号与领域知识，识别可迁移的机制性洞见，通过多模型生成与交叉评审产生有根据、可测试的研究计划；在Idea Execution阶段，协调智能体将计划分解为实验，迭代实现与诊断，并通过独立的基于证据的评审后才接受研究结论。

Result: 在RSICD基准上，AutoResearch生成的idea将平均Recall从32.84提升至34.69；在可靠性方面，仅记录5次审计确认的问题事件，远低于其他自主研究系统的11-27次，展现了更优的进度提升和结果可靠性。

Conclusion: AutoResearch证明了有意义的洞见在实验前被夯实、结论在接受前被验证的研究流程是可行且有效的，实现了"Insight In, Hallucination Out"（洞见输入，幻觉消除）的核心理念。

Abstract: Autonomous research systems are increasingly capable of executing long research workflows, yet automation alone does not ensure that the resulting process remains scientifically grounded. We introduce AutoResearch, a two-stage system that connects Idea Generation with Idea Execution to address both how research ideas are formed and how they are reliably established through experimentation. In Idea Generation, AutoResearch continuously integrates emerging research signals with accumulated domain knowledge, identifies transferable mechanistic insights, and uses multi-model generation and cross-review to produce grounded, testable research plans. In Idea Execution, coordinated agents decompose these plans into experiments, iteratively implement and diagnose them, and employ independent evidence-based review before accepting research conclusions. Across representative settings in cross-modal retrieval, systems optimization, and benchmark-driven machine learning, AutoResearch turns generated ideas into measurable progress, detects and corrects unreliable experimental results, and makes evidence-conditioned decisions to continue, revise, or terminate research directions. For example, on RSICD benchmark, an AutoResearch-generated idea improves mean Recall from 32.84 to 34.69, while recording only 5 audit-confirmed issue events compared with 11-27 for other autonomous research systems. These results demonstrate a research process in which meaningful insight is grounded before experimentation and conclusions are grounded before acceptance: Insight In, Hallucination Out.

</details>


### [146] [Adaptive Policy Portfolios for Robust Markov Decision Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17929)
*Kasper Engelen,Sebastian Junges,Guillermo A. Pérez,Marnix Suilen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了鲁棒马尔可夫决策过程中的自适应策略组合问题，即离线合成有限个无记忆随机策略，并搭配轻量级的在线选择器。作者从计算复杂性角度分析了策略组合的认证与合成的难度，证明认证问题对确定性策略组合是∀ℝ-完全的，而合成问题对一般有理多胞体是∃∀ℝ-完全的，并提出了可离线构建且支持运行时特化的策略组合方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 鲁棒MDP传统上是对一组可能的转移函数优化单一策略，但当未知动力学在部署后是固定的且可部分识别时，这种方法可能过于保守。因此需要一种能适应不同环境的自适应策略组合方案，同时需要理解其计算复杂性以指导实际应用。

Method: 1) 定义鲁棒后悔度作为策略组合质量的度量，衡量每个可行环境下组合中最佳成员相对于最优策略的损失；2) 从计算复杂性理论角度对策略组合的认证（验证给定组合的质量）和合成（构建最优组合）进行形式化分析；3) 提出一种离线策略组合构建方法，支持运行时特化。

Result: 1) 在无环(s,a)-矩形RMDP中，即使对确定性策略组合，认证问题也是∀ℝ-完全的；2) 对一般有理多胞体，合成单元限大小的策略组合是∃∀ℝ-完全的，即使固定折扣和无环动力学；3) 单策略情形在组合和代数层面都已具有计算困难性；4) 提出了可离线构造且支持运行时特化的策略组合方法。

Conclusion: 自适应策略组合的认证与合成都具有较高的计算复杂性（分别达到∀ℝ-完全和∃∀ℝ-完全），这表明精确求解这些问题在计算上代价高昂。尽管如此，作者提出的离线构造方法为运行时特化提供了可行的途径，兼顾了鲁棒性与计算可行性。

Abstract: Robust Markov decision processes optimize one policy against a set of plausible transition functions. This can be conservative when the unknown dynamics are fixed and become partially identifiable after deployment. We study adaptive policy portfolios: finite sets of memoryless randomized policies synthesized offline and paired with a lightweight online selector. Robust regret is a natural measure of portfolio quality: for each plausible environment, it measures the loss of the best portfolio member relative to the policy that would have been optimal had that environment been known. Related regret objectives were studied by Ghavamzadeh et al. (2016) with an emphasis on approximations and relaxations for safe policy improvement. We give a complexity-theoretic account of portfolio certification and synthesis. Certifying a given portfolio is $\forall\mathbb{R}$-complete already for deterministic portfolios in acyclic (s,a)-rectangular RMDPs. Synthesizing a portfolio of unary-bounded size is $\exists\forall\mathbb{R}$-complete for general rational polytopes, even with fixed discount and acyclic dynamics. The single-policy case is already hard, both combinatorially and algebraically. Finally, we present an offline portfolio construction that is amenable to runtime specialization.

</details>


### [147] [EvoTS-Agent: A Self-Evolving LLM Agent for Financial Time Series Change Point Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17933)
*Lei Jiang,Ye Wei,Xinyu Xi,Jordan Langham-Lopez,Yifan Bao,Raad Khraishi,Yihao Ang,Anthony K. H. Tung,Lukasz Szpruch,Hao Ni*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出EvoTS-Agent，一个基于验证反馈驱动的自进化LLM代理，用于金融时序变点检测。通过三种进化算子（修订、替代策略、重组）自动构建检测流程，在四个基准数据集上超越现有LLM代理，且执行成功率100%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融时间序列具有非平稳性和异质性统计特性，导致变点检测极具挑战性：没有单一无监督算法能跨资产和市场制度保持一致表现。传统工作流严重依赖专家进行模型选择、特征设计和超参数调优，限制了可扩展性和适应性。

Method: EvoTS-Agent包含三个核心步骤：(1) 进行策划性探索性数据分析以表征数据集属性并初始化候选检测模型；(2) 通过三种互补算子进化可执行的实验轨迹——Revision利用当前最优解，Alternative Strategy在进展停滞时探索根本不同的建模方向，Recombination综合高表现轨迹的互补证据；(3) 验证反馈引导轨迹进化，使代理适应每个数据集统计特性并保持可靠优化。

Result: 在四个基准数据集上的实验表明，EvoTS-Agent持续优于现有基于LLM的代理，并在所有评估的后端LLM上保持100%的执行成功率。

Conclusion: 验证引导的自进化机制有效提升了LLM代理在金融变点检测任务上的自主性和适应性，同时保证了流程执行的可靠性。

Abstract: Financial time series exhibit non-stationary and heterogeneous statistical properties, making change-point detection challenging because no single unsupervised algorithm performs consistently across assets and market regimes. Conventional workflows consequently depend heavily on expert-driven model selection, feature design, and hyperparameter tuning, limiting their scalability and adaptability. We propose EvoTS-Agent, a validation-guided self-evolving LLM agent for autonomous financial time-series change-point detection. EvoTS-Agent first performs curated exploratory data analysis to characterize dataset properties and initialize candidate detection models. It then evolves executable experiment trajectories through three complementary operators: \textit{Revision} exploits the current best solution, \textit{Alternative Strategy} explores fundamentally different modeling directions when progress stagnates, and \textit{Recombination} synthesizes complementary evidence from high-performing trajectories. Validation feedback guides trajectory evolution throughout the search, enabling the agent to adapt its detection pipeline to the statistical characteristics of each dataset while preserving reliable optimization. Experiments across four benchmark datasets demonstrate that EvoTS-Agent consistently outperforms existing LLM-based agents while maintaining a 100\% execution success rate across all evaluated backbone LLMs.

</details>


### [148] [Procedural Content Metageneration via Program Search and Continual Abstraction Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17947)
*Matthew Siper,Ahmed Khalifa,Julian Togelius*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究利用大语言模型在程序生成器层面（而非单个关卡层面）进行程序化内容生成搜索。作者提出"持续抽象发现"（CAD）方法，从高适应度程序中提取可复用原语到辅助模块中。在四个游戏领域（Sokoban、Zelda、Dangerous Dave、Lode Runner）的160次完整演化运行中，CAD在所有八组领域与API对比中均提升了平均最终适应度，证明发现可复用原语能改进内容生成器的演化程序搜索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统程序化内容生成通常搜索单个关卡，但大语言模型能直接生成可执行程序，使得在程序生成器层面进行搜索成为可能。然而，如何让演化搜索更高效、如何利用演化过程中发现的有用抽象原语，是一个亟待探索的问题。

Method: 提出Continual Abstraction Discovery（CAD）方法，通过语言模型的变异和交叉操作演化完整的Python生成器。CAD从高适应度程序中提取可复用原语，存入运行专属的辅助模块。实验采用2x2设计，交叉比较CAD与固定手写领域API的访问权限，在四个游戏领域中进行至少10次、共50代的演化运行，共完成160次完整运行。

Result: CAD在所有八个领域与API组合对比中均提升了平均最终最佳适应度。在CAD运行中，学习到的库被大多数后续程序采纳，并反复发现了验证、可达性和结构相关的实用工具。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，在演化程序搜索中主动发现和复用可重用原语，能够显著提升内容生成器的演化效率和质量，是一种有效的改进方向。

Abstract: Large language models can generate executable programs, which makes it possible to search directly over procedural content generators rather than individual levels. We study this approach in Sokoban, Zelda, Dangerous Dave, and Lode Runner. Each run evolves complete Python generators through language-model mutation and crossover. We introduce Continual Abstraction Discovery, or CAD, which extracts reusable primitives from high-fitness programs into a run-specific helper module. A 2x2 experiment crosses CAD with access to a fixed hand-written domain API. The completed data set contains 160 complete runs, with at least ten 50-generation runs in every cell. CAD raises mean final best fitness in all eight domain and API comparisons. Across all CAD runs, learned libraries are adopted by most later programs and repeatedly rediscover validation, reachability, and structural utilities. These results support that discovering reusable primitives improves evolutionary program search for content generators.

</details>


### [149] [Towards Zero-Shot Task Transfer with Neurosymbolic World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17959)
*Isidoro Tamassia,Lennert De Smet,Giuseppe Marra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新型的神经符号世界模型（neurosymbolic world model），将奖励预测与观测重构解耦，使世界模型能够零样本适应新任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于模型强化学习方法虽然学习神经网络世界模型具有强大表达能力，但其潜在表示与训练任务绑定、不可解释、难以泛化到新任务。

Method: 提出将奖励预测仅依赖于潜在状态中结构化的符号子集，实现观测重构与奖励预测的解耦，从而学习可跨任务泛化的神经符号世界模型。

Result: 实验证明该方法在零样本适应新奖励函数方面表现出比纯神经方法更强的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过符号化结构分解，世界模型可以实现任务无关的潜在表示和零样本迁移，但该方向仍面临若干挑战，值得进一步探索。

Abstract: State-of-the-art model-based reinforcement learning methods learn neural world models that allow policy improvement by planning in a latent space, without assumptions on the structure of the underlying environment. While expressive, these models are generally task-dependent: they learn uninterpretable latent representations that are tied to the training task and thus hard to generalize to new tasks. In this work, we present a novel world model formulation where the reward prediction only depends on a subset of structured, symbolic components of the whole latent state. Decoupling observation reconstruction and reward prediction allows us to learn world models that can adapt zero-shot, i.e. without further environment interactions, to new reward functions defined over the same symbolic state space. We discuss the main advantages and challenges of learning these neurosymbolic world models and demonstrate the strong generalisation properties of our approach over purely neural methods.

</details>


### [150] [Can Large Language Models Explain Flight Safety Events? A Prior-Guided Semantic LLM-based Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18017)
*Lu Xu,Xu Li,Linjiang Zheng,Fan Li,Riquan Zhang,Jiaxing Shang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出FlightLLM，一种基于先验引导的语义大语言模型方法，用于可解释的飞行安全分析。通过特征工程解决模态不一致、语义离散化模块将数值模式转换为定性描述、引入CatBoost作为统计专家注入先验、采用对比少样本学习应对数据稀缺，并在Prompt中嵌入航空领域知识，最终在704个真实A320航班样本上实现了有竞争力的分类性能和直接的因果解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可解释AI技术（如特征重要性图）需要大量领域知识才能转化为有操作意义的解释，而LLM具备语言推理能力，有望弥合这一鸿沟。但在该领域应用LLM面临模态不一致、分类能力有限、微调数据稀缺和缺乏领域知识等挑战。

Method: 1) 特征工程：组合统计描述符与物理意义的飞行指标以解决模态不一致；2) 语义离散化模块：将抽象数值模式转换为定性描述；3) 引入CatBoost作为统计专家，将预测结果作为先验注入Prompt；4) 采用对比少样本学习策略应对数据不足；5) 设计结构化Prompt嵌入航空特定知识。

Result: 在包含704个真实A320航班样本（以重着陆为锚定事件）的数据集上，FlightLLM实现了有竞争力的分类性能，同时生成了直接且合理的事件原因解释。

Conclusion: FlightLLM成功地将LLM的语言推理能力与统计模型的分类能力相结合，在飞行安全分析中实现了既准确又可解释的风险事件分析，为飞行数据驱动的事故归因提供了新范式。

Abstract: Improving flight safety with flight data requires not only accurate detection of risk events, but more importantly, clear interpretation of their underlying causes at the level of pilot control behavior. Existing explainable AI techniques, such as feature importance maps, often require considerable domain knowledge to translate them into operationally meaningful explanations. Large Language Models (LLMs), which excel at language reasoning, bring a promising solution to this issue. However, applying LLMs in this domain presents key challenges such as modal inconsistency, limited classification ability, scarcity of task-specific data for fine-tuning, and lack of domain knowledge. To overcome these challenges, we propose FlightLLM, a prior-guided semantic LLM-based approach for interpretable flight safety analysis. Specifically, we first perform feature engineering to address modal inconsistency, combining statistical descriptors with physically meaningful flight indicators. This representation is further processed by a Semantic Discretization module, which converts abstract numerical patterns into qualitative descriptions that are more compatible with language reasoning. In addition, since LLMs are not inherently strong classifiers, CatBoost is incorporated as a statistical expert, and its prediction results are injected into the prompt as prior guidance. A contrastive few-shot learning strategy is further adopted to compensate for limited data. Finally, we design structured prompts to embed aviation-specific knowledge into the inference process. Using hard landing, a representative risk event with complex causal mechanisms, as an anchor point, we evaluate FlightLLM on a dataset of 704 real-world A320 flight samples. Experimental results show that the proposed approach achieves competitive classification performance while generating direct and reasonable explanations for event causes.

</details>


### [151] [StagedWorkspace: A Versioned Workspace for Knowledge-Work Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18050)
*Yining Hua,Hongbin Na,Yifan Zhou,Akshay Kalose,Cyrus Ayubcha,Levi Lian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出StagedWorkspace，一个面向知识工作智能体的版本化工作空间，将解析视图和审查差异绑定到原生文件的哈希版本上，解决AI智能体在处理代码、文档、表格等数字工件时版本不一致的问题。实验表明该方法在多个基准上显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI智能体日益承担知识工作（生成和修改代码库、文档、表格、幻灯片等持久数字工件），但它们在搜索时解析的视图、编辑的原生文件、审查的变更和提交的工件可能指向同一工作产品的不同版本。现有的编码智能体通过仓库契约（search、diffs、tests）部分解决了这一问题，但对于PDF、电子表格、幻灯片、笔记和混合格式项目文件夹，类似契约不够明确。因此需要建立工作空间状态契约。

Method: 提出StagedWorkspace，一个版本化工作空间，将解析记录和审查差异绑定到原生文件的哈希值上，随文件变化而更新。系统提供双重的解析/原生访问能力，并在固定测试框架下进行消融实验，评估不同访问视图对智能体性能的影响。

Result: 在OfficeQA Pro和APEX-Agents基准上，双重的解析/原生访问在所有测试模型中取得最高点估计值；相比单一视图，OfficeQA Pass@1提升8.3-12.1个点，APEX平均评分提升4.7-9.2个点。SW-AGENT在OfficeQA上以Gemini 3.1 Pro达到63.9%，在APEX上以GPT-5.4 Nano达到42.1，而发布版同模型分数分别为29.3%和25.5。在57个文件编辑任务的配对审查轴消融中，可见差异时得分更高。

Conclusion: 工作空间状态应被视为知识工作智能体中的实验变量。成果表明版本化工作空间能显著提升智能体性能，并促使未来基准应将对证据、分阶段编辑和提交工件作为显式状态转换进行评分。

Abstract: AI agents increasingly perform knowledge work (i.e., produce and modify persistent digital artifacts such as code repositories, documents, spreadsheets, slides, reports), yet the parsed views they search, the native files they edit, the changes they review, and the artifacts they submit can refer to different versions of the same work product. We formulate this as a workspace-state contract: every view should be explicitly tied to a version of the evolving workspace state. Coding agents partly address this need through repository contracts for search, diffs, and tests, whereas an analogous contract is less explicit for PDFs, spreadsheets, slides, notebooks, and mixed-format project folders. We propose StagedWorkspace, a versioned workspace for knowledge-work agents. The workspace binds parsed records and review diffs to content hashes of the native files as they change. In fixed-harness ablations on OfficeQA Pro and APEX-Agents, dual parsed/native access has the highest point estimate for every tested model; relative to the more limiting single view, it improves OfficeQA Pass@1 by 8.3-12.1 points and APEX mean rubric score by 4.7-9.2 points. SW-AGENT scores 63.9% with Gemini 3.1 Pro on OfficeQA and 42.1 with GPT-5.4 Nano on APEX, compared with published same-model scores of 29.3% and 25.5, respectively. A paired review-axis ablation on 57 file-editing tasks further finds higher observed scores when diffs are visible. These results identify workspace state as an experimental variable in knowledge-work agents and motivate benchmarks that score evidence, staged edits, and submitted artifacts as explicit state transitions.

</details>


### [152] [HLSR: Hybrid Live Forecast Selective Dynamic Vehicle Rerouting for Real-Time Congestion Avoidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18056)
*Xiao Wang,Shun Ren Yang,Hui Nien Hung*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出HLSR框架，一种选择性的混合实时-预测车辆重路由方法，通过融合实时边缘速度与短期预测，在有限的干预范围内实现网络级出行时间最短路径重路由，避免了传统方法需要所有车辆频繁重规划的假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市交通拥堵降低生产力并增加出行成本与排放。虽然网络级实时出行时间最短路径重路由在仿真中效果显著，但现有方法假设每辆车在每个决策周期都被重新规划，这一假设在实际部署中不现实。

Method: HLSR基于双重阈值拥堵检测、校准的上游选择以及面向驾驶员定制的出行时间预测，进一步引入接近车辆扩展、出行时间加权k最短路径生成，以及依赖时间视野的混合实时-预测路段速度用于多成本路径分配。

Result: 论文通过仿真验证了HLSR在有限干预范围下能够高效实现选择性重路由效果（具体数值结果未在本摘要中给出）。

Conclusion: HLSR通过选择性干预和混合实时-预测信息融合，在更现实的可部署条件下实现了有效的网络级车辆重路由，平衡了干预成本与路网效率。

Abstract: Urban traffic congestion reduces productivity and increases travel cost and emissions. Network-wide live travel-time shortest-path rerouting can be highly effective in simulation, but assumes that essentially every on-road vehicle is replanned every decision period. We propose HLSR, a selective hybrid live--forecast vehicle rerouting framework that fuses live edge speeds with short-horizon forecasts under limited intervention scope. Building on dual-threshold congestion detection, calibrated upstream selection, and driver-tailored travel-time prediction, HLSR further introduces approaching-vehicle expansion, travel-time-weighted k-shortest-path generation, and a horizon-dependent hybrid live--forecast segment speed used in multi-cost route allocation.

</details>


### [153] [Delegation Asymmetry in Agentic Recommender Systems: Measuring Two-Sided Receptivity in Online Dating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18058)
*Daria Leshchikova,Valentina V. Kuskova,Dmitry Zaytsev,Valerii Klimov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究通过两大规模调查（N=2,894和N=2,617）表明，用户对代理通信的"发送意愿"和"接收意愿"是两个高度相关但可区分的构造（rho=0.92，ΔBIC=52），并揭示了一个系统性的委托不对称：部署自己的代理（阈值-0.38）远比参与对方的代理（+0.32，完全参与+1.39）更容易接受。在随机配对的反事实下，仅有4-13%的有向二元组同时具备代理部署和接收者参与。设计反事实分析表明：互惠要求会削减一半以上的交互量，而基于接收意愿路由代理联系可使每次接触的参与度提升三倍（AUC=0.88）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主LLM代理作为用户代表进行对话是一种新兴设计模式，但其可行性依赖一个很少被检验的条件：用户不仅需要接受将对话委托给代理，还必须接受来自他人的代理中介通信。作者旨在系统性地研究这一条件，量化用户对代理通信的接受度及其对匹配平台设计的影响。

Method: 基于两个大型在线调查（分别在生成式档案特征和自主对话代理两个主题上，覆盖两种语言），开发了基于分级响应模型和潜在回归的潜在变量测量模型来度量代理接受度。通过模型比较检验发送与接收意愿的构造区分性，并利用随机配对反事实和设计反事实量化不同设计选择的影响。采用预留目标项的响应者级交叉验证评估路由策略的泛化性能。

Result: 发送和接收代理通信的意愿是高度相关（rho=0.92）但可分离（ΔBIC=52）的构造，且跨语言具有部分测量不变性。存在明显的委托不对称：部署自己代理的阈值远低于参与他人代理，平均部署倾向约为参与倾向的三倍。随机配对下仅4-13%的有向二元组能组合部署与接收，存在性别方向性失衡。互惠要求排除近三分之二的潜在部署，将交互量削减一半以上；而基于接收意愿的路由策略使单次接触参与度提升三倍，并通过了样本外验证（AUC=0.88，3.1x四分位提升）。

Conclusion: 代理通信接受度是匹配平台设计的关键维度，"发送"与"接收"必须作为独立构造来建模。部署与接收之间的不对称意味着简单地允许用户部署代理不足以产生有效匹配，需要设计感知接受度的路由机制和披露/选择加入机制。研究为agentic推荐系统设计提供了量化依据，包括披露策略、选择加入机制和接受度感知的匹配算法。

Abstract: Autonomous LLM agents that converse on a user's behalf are an emerging design pattern in matching platforms, yet their viability depends on a condition rarely examined: users must accept not only delegating conversation to an agent, but also receiving agent-mediated communication from others. We study this condition using two large-scale surveys of active users of a major dating platform (N=2,894 on generative profile features; N=2,617 on autonomous conversational agents, fielded in two languages). We develop a latent-variable measurement model of agent receptivity based on graded response models with latent regression, and show via model comparison that willingness to send and willingness to receive agent communication are distinct constructs: highly correlated (rho=0.92) but separable (Delta BIC=52), with partial measurement invariance across languages. The model quantifies a systematic delegation asymmetry: deploying one's own agent requires far lower receptivity (threshold -0.38) than engaging a counterpart's agent (+0.32; full engagement +1.39), and mean deployment propensity exceeds engagement propensity roughly threefold. Under a random-pairing counterfactual derived from stated receptivity, only 4-13% of directed dyads combine agent deployment with receiver engagement, with a pronounced gender-directional imbalance. Design counterfactuals quantify the levers: a reciprocity requirement cuts interaction volume by half or more by excluding nearly two-thirds of would-be deployment, while routing agent contacts on receive receptivity triples per-contact engagement, a lift that survives out-of-sample validation with the target item held out (AUC 0.88, 3.1x quartile lift under respondent-level cross-validation). We discuss implications for agentic recommender design, including disclosure, opt-in mechanics, and receptivity-aware matchmaking.

</details>


### [154] [On the Fragility of Self-Improving Agents: Variance, Task Order, and Underspecification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18066)
*Qinyuan Ye,Yu Li,Yada Pruksachatkun,Jiaxin Zhang,Chien-Sheng Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对基于记忆的自我改进智能体进行了全面重新评估，发现这些方法在评估噪声和任务顺序依赖两个方面存在严重脆弱性，并通过实验表明任务和环境规格不充分是导致其脆弱性的重要因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 记忆型自我改进智能体虽在近期文献中展现出潜力，但其可靠性问题被严重忽视。现有评估方法存在缺陷，未充分考虑评估噪声和任务顺序对性能的影响。

Method: 对两种基于记忆的方法进行重新评估，从两个维度扩展评估范围：（1）多次运行以量化方差；（2）随机打乱任务顺序以考察任务顺序影响。同时人工检查智能体记忆并提出规格不充分的假设，通过加入详细评分标准和环境反馈等额外信息进行验证。

Result: 发现两大问题：一是智能体评估在复杂环境和多步任务中天然噪杂，自我改进循环会放大噪声；二是智能体改进高度依赖任务顺序，默认顺序隐含课程安排，成为成功的前提条件。加入额外规格信息只能部分弥补性能下降，仍有显著差距。

Conclusion: 呼吁采用更严格的评估协议（多轮次结果报告、压力测试），并强调需要有效的人类监督系统与接口，防止智能体以不可预见的方式失败。

Abstract: Memory-based self-improving agents--those that learn from an online stream of tasks and improve over time by maintaining a textual memory bank--have shown great promise in recent literature. However, the reliability aspects of these methods have been critically overlooked. In this work, we conduct a comprehensive re-evaluation of two memory-based methods, broadening the scope of evaluation along two axes: (1) including multiple runs to quantify variance, and (2) randomly shuffling the tasks to investigate the effect of task order. Through these experiments, we make two observations that expose the fragility of current methods: First, agent evaluation is inherently noisy in complex environments and on multi-step tasks, and stacking a self-improving loop on top can further amplify this noise. Second, the agent's improvement is highly dependent on task order. Prior works often adopt default orderings that impose an implicit curriculum, acting as a hidden prerequisite for success.
  To better understand this fragility, we manually examine the agents' memory and hypothesize that task and environment underspecification contribute to this fragility. We validate this hypothesis by incorporating information that enables better specification, such as detailed rubrics and environment feedback, into the memory construction process. While this added information partially closes the performance degradation in previous experiments, significant gaps still remain, suggesting that other uncharacterized factors contribute to this fragility. Looking ahead, our work advocates for more rigorous evaluation protocols for self-improving agents by reporting results across multiple runs and stress-testing them under challenging conditions. Moreover, our findings on underspecification call for systems and interfaces that enable effective human oversight, preventing agents from failing in unforeseeable ways.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [155] [Proactive Road Safety Intervention in Australia: Predicting Risky Driving Hotspots from Connected Vehicle Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16913)
*Adriana-Simona Mihăiţă,Clarence Cheung,Artur Grigorev,Tuo Mao,David Lillo-Trynes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文利用澳大利亚大悉尼地区的网联车辆遥测数据，通过g力阈值量化危险驾驶行为（急刹车、急转弯、急加速），在地方政府区域（LGA）层面构建时空热力图识别高风险区域，并对比了八种预测模型（集成学习、深度学习和经典时间序列方法）。结果显示ARIMA表现最佳（MAE: 162.21），与LSTM相当，且优于所有集成方法，表明在数据量有限的情况下，简约时间序列模型与深度学习具有竞争力。悉尼内城和西部LGA（CBD、Parramatta、Bankstown）被识别为持续高风险区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统道路安全监测是被动的，依赖事故记录分析，无法在伤亡发生前识别高风险位置和危险驾驶行为。本文旨在利用网联车辆遥测数据主动检测和预测近碰撞风险驾驶事件，填补这一关键但探索不足的研究空白。

Method: 使用g力阈值（急刹车>0.6g、急转弯>0.47g、急加速>0.5g）量化危险驾驶事件，构建时空热力图识别高风险区域，并在LGA层面基准测试八种预测模型：集成学习（随机森林、XGBoost、LightGBM）、深度学习（LSTM、N-BEATS）和经典时间序列方法（ARIMA、指数平滑、Prophet）。

Result: ARIMA取得最低平均绝对误差（MAE: 162.21），与LSTM（MAE: 163.92）表现相当，优于所有集成方法，N-BEATS的MAE为180.75。悉尼的CBD、Parramatta和Bankstown被识别为持续高风险区域。

Conclusion: 在训练数据量有限的情况下，简约时间序列模型（ARIMA）与深度学习模型具有竞争力，甚至表现更好。该研究展示了基于IoT的网联车辆数据在支持主动道路安全干预方面的潜力，并为高风险区域的政策制定提供了数据驱动的依据。

Abstract: Road safety monitoring has historically been reactive, relying on crash-record analysis after fatalities and injuries have already occurred. Proactive identification of high-risk locations and dangerous driving behaviour before incidents occur is a critical but underexplored challenge. This paper addresses this gap using connected vehicle telemetry data from Greater Sydney, Australia, to detect and forecast near-miss risky driving events at the Local Government Area (LGA) level. Risky driving is quantified through g-force thresholds (hard braking >0.6g, harsh cornering >0.47g, harsh acceleration >0.5g), and spatio-temporal heatmaps are constructed to identify high-risk zones. Eight predictive models are benchmarked across three families: ensemble learning (Random Forests, XGBoost, LightGBM), deep learning (LSTM, N-BEATS), and classical time-series methods (ARIMA, Exponential Smoothing, Prophet). ARIMA achieves the lowest mean absolute error (MAE: 162.21), performing comparably to LSTM (MAE: 163.92) and outperforming all ensemble methods, with N-BEATS reaching an MAE of 180.75. These results demonstrate that parsimonious time-series models are competitive with deep learning approaches when training data volume is limited. The study highlights the potential of IoT-based connected vehicle data to support proactive road safety interventions, with Sydney's inner and western LGAs (CBD, Parramatta, Bankstown) identified as persistent high-risk zones warranting targeted policy action.

</details>


### [156] [Detecting and Discriminating Operator Misspecification in Hybrid PDE-Parameter Learning: a Reference-Free Instrument, with Discrimination Bounded In Sample](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16925)
*Eric Fock*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文构建了一种仪器，能够从单次拟合中（无需oracle）区分混合PDE参数估计器所假设的算子是否错误，以及参数是否仅不可辨识。在自伴抛物型反问题中，信息矩阵统计量在正确设定下中位数为0.19，误报率0.033低于0.10的预设上限，而在两种错误设定下分别升至224和85并全部触发。在正确但不可辨识的设计中该统计量保持沉默（0.050），而秩统计量在预设边界处塌缩至零。该分离能力是核心贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常规准确性检查具有盲区：错误设定的估计器域内RMSE为2.7×10⁻²，低于观测噪声，而系数误差在零噪声和最大噪声下分别达29.7%和31.2%，无法被传统指标察觉。需要一种工具能从单次拟合中区分模型错误与参数不可辨识这两种不同的失败模式。

Method: 构建了一个信息矩阵统计量，配以plug-in尺度和每种子参数，通过单次拟合同时读取两种诊断信息。设计包括三个实验设置：正确设定、两种错误设定、以及正确但不可辨识的设计。采用预注册的阈值和Clopper-Pearson置信区间，并与秩统计量相结合进行判定。

Result: 正确设定下统计量中位数0.19，拒绝率0.033（低于0.10上限）；两种错误设定下分别升至224和85，所有重复全部触发。不可辨识设计中统计量保持0.050（Clopper-Pearson区间[0.024,0.090]），而秩统计量在边界c₅*=2.15×10⁻³处塌缩到零。此外，不同架构（单参数曲线拟合、裸参数、49/241参数MLP）收敛到同一伪真值（闭合形式匹配0.07%），而物理信息网络收敛到不同值。

Conclusion: 该分离能力（而非仅检测）是本文的核心贡献。仪器在样本内是界，样本外是方向。但也报告了其盲区、预注册的负结果（神经估计器在恢复任务中败给Tikhonov正则化反演），以及其保证成立但训练网络违反该保证的假设条件。

Abstract: We build an instrument that reads, from a single fit and with no oracle, whether the operator a hybrid PDE-parameter estimator postulates is wrong-and separates that from a merely unidentifiable parameter. On one self-adjoint parabolic inverse problem, an information-matrix statistic with plug-in scale and per-seed parameter has median 0.19 under correct specification, rejection rate $0.033$ against a pre-registered ceiling of $0.10$, and rises to $224$ and $85$ under two misspecifications, firing in every replicate. On a correctly specified but non-identifiable design it stays mute-$0.050$ at $n=200$, Clopper-Pearson $[0.024, 0.090]$-while a rank statistic collapses to zero at a pre-registered boundary $c_5^*=2.15\times10^{-3}.$ Two readings of one fit therefore separate the two failures across the three designs a deployable test reaches. That separation is the contribution; detection alone is a crowded flank. In sample it is a bound, out of sample a direction. It is needed because the usual accuracy check is blind: the misspecified estimator's in-domain RMSE is $2.7\times 10^{-2}$, below the observation noise for $σ\geq 0.05,$ while the coefficient is wrong by $29.7\%$ at zero noise, $31.2\%$ at the loudest. Nor is the failure architectural: a one-parameter curve fit, a bare parameter and multilayer perceptrons of $49$ and $241$ parameters converge to the same pseudo-true, matched in closed form to $0.07\%,$ whereas a physics-informed network, with its composite objective, converges to a disjoint one. We report where the instrument is blind, a pre-registered negative where a neural estimator loses to Tikhonov-regularized inversion at recovery, and the hypothesis under which its guarantee holds but a trained network violates it.

</details>


### [157] [Data-DPO: Direct Preference Optimization for Target Model Data Selection in LLM Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16926)
*Peng Sun,Yi Yang,Antong Zhang,Chunxiao Li,Yanbo Wang,Dianbo Liu,xin chen,Kai Yu,Lu Chen,Tianfan Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Data-DPO，一种面向目标模型的SFT数据选择方法。通过一步探测观察目标模型的局部训练反馈，将样本间的激活差异转化为成对数据偏好，训练轻量级奖励模型来学习目标模型感知的数据偏好，并结合目标模型偏好、外部质量分数和边际多样性构建更稳定的训练子集。实验表明在Vision-Flan和LLaVA-CoT上优于现有基线并超越全量数据训练性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据选择方法通常将数据价值视为静态属性，忽视了数据与目标模型能力分布之间的兼容性，导致所选数据难以充分发挥目标模型的潜力。

Method: Data-DPO包含三个关键步骤：1) 通过一步探测观察目标模型在不同样本上的局部训练反馈；2) 将样本间的激活差异转化为成对数据偏好，训练轻量级奖励模型学习目标模型感知的数据偏好；3) 在最终选择阶段，结合目标模型偏好、外部质量分数和边际多样性构建更稳定有效的训练子集。

Result: 在Vision-Flan和LLaVA-CoT数据集上，Data-DPO在多种数据预算下一致优于现有数据选择基线方法，并能稳定超越全量数据训练的性能。

Conclusion: Data-DPO通过将目标模型的能力分布纳入数据选择过程，有效解决了数据选择中模型-数据兼容性问题，在降低训练成本的同时提升了模型性能，验证了目标模型感知的动态数据选择方法的有效性。

Abstract: Data selection in supervised fine-tuning aims to select a small set of effective samples from large-scale candidate data, reducing training cost while preserving model performance. However, existing methods usually treat data value as a relatively static property, and pay limited attention to the compatibility between data and the capability distribution of the target model. To address this issue, we propose Data-DPO, a target model-oriented SFT data selection method. Data-DPO observes the local training feedback of the target model on different samples through one-step probing, transforms activation differences among samples into pairwise data preferences, and trains a lightweight reward model to learn target-model-aware data preferences. In the final selection stage, Data-DPO further combines target model preference, external quality scores, and marginal diversity to construct a more stable and effective training subset. Experimental results on Vision-Flan and LLaVA-CoT show that Data-DPO consistently outperforms existing data selection baselines under multiple data budgets and stably surpasses full data training performance.

</details>


### [158] [Hierarchical Data Selection via Manifold Coverage and Sparse Feature Coverage in LLM Post-training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16927)
*Peng Sun,Yi Yang,Antong Zhang,Chunxiao Li,Yanbo Wang,Dianbo Liu,xin chen,Kai Yu,Lu Chen,Tianfan Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MASS方法，将数据选择建模为从粗到细的层次覆盖问题，通过密集自编码器学习低维主流行坐标进行粗粒度语义分组，再利用TopK稀疏自编码器在组内进行质量感知的稀疏特征覆盖，在Vision Flan和LLaVA-CoT上显著优于现有数据选择基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着监督微调数据规模不断扩大，如何从大规模候选中选择高价值子集以降低训练成本并提升模型性能至关重要。现有方法直接在原始嵌入空间中测量多样性，几何度量会混淆主导语义方向、细粒度监督差异和局部噪声，导致数据选择效果受限。

Method: MASS将数据选择形式化为从粗到细的层次覆盖问题。首先使用密集自编码器学习低维主流行坐标，实现粗粒度语义分组；然后在每个组内使用TopK稀疏自编码器进行质量感知的稀疏特征覆盖，从而兼顾全局语义多样性与局部细粒度差异。

Result: 在Vision Flan和LLaVA-CoT上，MASS在多个预算设置下一致优于强数据选择基线，并且在若干场景下仅使用少量数据子集即可匹配甚至超越全量数据训练的性能。

Conclusion: MASS通过层次化的粗到细覆盖策略有效解决了原始嵌入空间中多样性度量混杂的问题，证明结合主流行坐标和稀疏特征覆盖能够在低数据预算下达到甚至超越全量训练的效果。

Abstract: As supervised fine-tuning data continues to scale, selecting high-value subsets from large candidate pools is crucial for reducing training cost and improving model performance. Existing methods often measure diversity directly in the original embedding space, where geometric metrics entangle dominant semantic directions, fine-grained supervision differences, and local noise. We address this limitation by formulating data selection as a coarse-to-fine hierarchical coverage problem and propose MASS. MASS learns low-dimensional principal manifold coordinates with a dense autoencoder for coarse semantic grouping, and then performs quality-aware sparse feature coverage within each group using a TopK sparse autoencoder. Experiments on Vision Flan and LLaVA-CoT show that MASS consistently outperforms strong data selection baselines across multiple budgets, and in several settings matches or surpasses full data training with only a small subset of data.

</details>


### [159] [Benchmarking Classical and Transformer-Based Models for Document Sensitivity Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16928)
*Aleesha Zainab,Muhammad Ahmed Khalid,Faheem Ullah Khan,Asifullah Khan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Strategic 16K，一个基于WikiLeaks PlusD外交电文的、经过泄漏控制的16,000篇文档敏感性分类基准数据集，并系统性地评估了六种模型架构。研究发现标签泄漏（label leakage）是敏感性分类中普遍但被低估的问题，本文通过扩展的泄漏去除协议消除了三类残留分类标记。在清洁基准上，BERT表现最佳（Accuracy=89.14%, F1=89.33%），TF-IDF+逻辑回归在经典模型中表现最强且计算成本更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组织文档的自动敏感性分类是一个关键但服务不足的问题，误分类可能导致监管违规或安全漏洞。基于AI的方法虽然可扩展，但其可靠性取决于训练数据的完整性。领域内普遍存在但未被充分报告的标签泄漏问题——文档正文中残留的分类标记使模型利用表面捷径而非学习真实的内容敏感性信号，导致性能估计被夸大且不可靠。因此需要一个泄漏控制条件下的可靠基准来正确评估模型性能。

Method: 本文构建了Strategic 16K数据集，从WikiLeaks PlusD中选取16,000篇外交电文，并设计了扩展的泄漏去除协议，识别并消除三类嵌入在文档正文中的残留分类标记。在此基础上，系统性地基准测试了六种模型架构，涵盖经典机器学习方法（如TF-IDF+逻辑回归）和基于Transformer的方法（如BERT、ELECTRA），在泄漏控制的条件下评估各模型的敏感性分类性能。

Result: 在清洁基准上，BERT表现最佳（Accuracy=89.14%, F1=89.33%），其次是ELECTRA（Accuracy=88.57%, F1=88.90%）。在经典模型中，TF-IDF结合逻辑回归在显著降低计算成本的同时实现了最强性能。这些结果是在明确泄漏控制条件下从WikiLeaks PlusD构建的首个完全可复现的敏感性分类基准。

Conclusion: 本文首次在明确泄漏控制的条件下，从WikiLeaks PlusD构建了完全可复现的文档敏感性分类基准。研究凸显了标签泄漏对模型性能评估的重大影响，并提供了有效的泄漏去除协议。结果验证了Transformer模型在该任务上的领先优势，同时表明经典模型在计算资源受限场景下仍具竞争力，为该领域后续研究提供了可靠的基础。

Abstract: Automatic sensitivity classification of organizational documents is a critical yet underserved problem, where the consequences of misclassification range from regulatory violations to security breaches. While AI-based approaches offer a scalable alternative to manual review, their reliability depends fundamentally on the integrity of training data. A pervasive but underreported problem in this domain is label leakage: residual classification markers embedded within document bodies that allow models to exploit surface shortcuts rather than learning genuine content-based sensitivity signals, producing performance estimates that are inflated and unreliable. This paper addresses this problem by introducing Strategic 16K, a carefully constructed, leakage-controlled corpus of 16,000 diplomatic cables sourced from the WikiLeaks Public Library of US Diplomacy (PlusD), and presents a systematic benchmark evaluating six model architectures spanning classical machine learning and transformer-based approaches. We document an extended leakage removal protocol that identifies and eliminates three categories of residual classification markers embedded within document bodies. On the clean benchmark, BERT achieves the strongest performance (Accuracy = 89.14%, F1 = 89.33%), followed by ELECTRA (Accuracy = 88.57%, F1 = 88.90%). Among classical models, TF-IDF with Logistic Regression achieves the strongest performance at significantly lower computational cost. These results constitute the first fully reproducible sensitivity classification benchmark constructed under explicit leakage-controlled conditions from WikiLeaks PlusD.

</details>


### [160] [Mr.Dec: Daily-Scale Longitudinal Multimodal Modeling for 30-Day Readmission Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16929)
*Minjun Kim,Jong Hak Moon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Mr.Dec多模态读入院风险预测解码器，将每次入院建模为每日多模态事件的时间顺序序列，通过Transformer解码器整合每日EHR更新和间歇性胸片发现，显著提升30天再入院预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将复杂的纵向住院史压缩为固定表示，损失了反映患者生理状态演变的日级临床信号，无法捕捉临床风险随证据积累的动态变化轨迹。

Method: 使用Transformer Decoder将每日EHR更新和间歇性CXR发现按时间对齐整合为序列流；采用疾病特异性监督对比学习作为辅助正则化，在潜在空间中诱导诊断感知结构；识别入院内的关键日提供可操作解释。

Result: 在MIMIC-IV和MIMIC-CXR数据集上达到最先进性能；模型能识别入院内关键日，为实时风险分层提供临床可解释的洞察。

Conclusion: 通过保留临床序列的完整性，Mr.Dec在30天再入院预测上优于现有方法，同时提供床边可操作的实时风险分层工具。

Abstract: Predicting 30-day hospital readmission is essential for assessing patient stability and optimizing healthcare resources. As clinical risk evolves with the accumulation of evidence during hospitalization, capturing these dynamic trajectories is essential. However, many existing approaches compress the complex longitudinal history into fixed representations, often losing the granular, day-level clinical signals that reflect a patient's evolving physiological state. To address this, we propose Mr.Dec (Multimodal Readmission-risk prediction Decoder), which models each admission as a natural chronological sequence of daily multimodal events. By leveraging a Transformer Decoder, Mr.Dec integrates daily Electronic Health Record(EHR) updates and intermittent Chest X-ray(CXR) findings in a time-aligned stream, reflecting the actual clinical workflow. To ensure robustness, we utilize Disease-Specific Supervised Contrastive Learning as an auxiliary regularization to induce a diagnosis-aware structure in the latent space. Evaluations on the MIMIC-IV and MIMIC-CXR datasets show that Mr.Dec achieves state-of-the-art performance by preserving the integrity of the clinical sequence. Furthermore, our model identifies "Critical Days" within an admission, providing actionable and clinically grounded interpretations for real-time risk stratification. Code is available at: https://github.com/yejix-ai/MR.DEC

</details>


### [161] [EMAN: Optimization-Driven Capacity Growth through Path Emergence in Multi-Task Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16930)
*Chenlei Fang,Jingchen Li,Hongzong LI,Qingyao Li,Yixuan Zhang,Huarui Wu,Haobin Shi,Chunjiang Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Emergent Modular Atomic Network (EMAN)，一种优化驱动的多任务学习框架。与依赖预定义结构或任务边界触发的现有方法不同，EMAN从精确的单路径计算开始，仅在持续优化证据出现时才动态生长出独立路径，避免了提前实例化第二路径，在PASCAL-Context和NYUv2等数据集上取得更优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多任务学习方法的能力变化通常受限于预定义结构，或由任务边界和冲突信号触发，缺乏真正自适应的动态扩展机制。作者希望回答一个根本性问题：网络能否从单路径计算出发，仅在出现持续优化证据时才生长出新的独立路径，从而实现更灵活、更高效的结构学习。

Method: EMAN框架包含两个核心机制：(1) 通过潜在相对相位暴露反对称增长方向，无需实例化第二条路径即可探测增长潜力；(2) 在训练过程中监控多个决策信号，将局部优化证据转化为结构决策。仅当得到认证后，EMAN才实例化两条等容量的独立路径，并自适应地分配共享与任务特定的表示容量。

Result: 在受控秩设置、PASCAL-Context和NYUv2基准上的大量实验验证了EMAN的有效性，在竞争性计算成本下取得了改进的性能。

Conclusion: EMAN展示了一种从单路径出发、由优化证据驱动的动态结构生长机制，为多任务学习中容量自适应的结构决策提供了新范式，兼顾了性能与计算效率。

Abstract: Existing multi-task learning methods rely on hard sharing, multiple paths or experts, adaptive sharing, and dynamic expansion. However, their capacity changes are usually constrained by predefined structures or triggered by task boundaries and conflict signals. This raises a fundamental question: can a network start from exact single-path computation and grow a new independent path only when persistent optimization evidence appears? We propose the Emergent Modular Atomic Network (EMAN), an optimization-driven framework for exposing an antisymmetric growth direction through latent relative phases without instantiating a second path, and for monitoring multiple decision signals during training to transform local optimization evidence into a structural decision. EMAN materializes two equal-capacity independent paths only after certification. EMAN adaptively allocates shared and task-specific representation capacity to accommodate varying task requirements. Extensive experiments on controlled rank settings, PASCAL-Context, and NYUv2 validate its effectiveness, achieving improved performance at a competitive computational cost.

</details>


### [162] [SW-ProxyCE: Zero-Query Adversarial Transfer from Public EEG Encoders to Private Downstream Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16931)
*Linhua Cong,Dingkun Liu,Dongrui Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次研究了EEG基础模型部署中的对抗迁移攻击安全问题。在"公开编码器+私有下游模型"场景下，提出Shrinkage-Whitened Proxy Cross-Entropy (SW-ProxyCE)攻击框架，仅利用公开编码器和少量有标签参考集，通过白化类原型恢复任务决策几何结构，无需训练替代分类器即可生成可迁移的对抗样本。实验表明该攻击在三个EEG任务上有效，且优于任务无关的表示偏移攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EEG基础模型编码器的公开发布在促进下游开发的同时引入了新的安全风险：公开可用的表示可能使私有下游模型变得脆弱。现有研究尚未探索这一攻击场景，即公开编码器+私有下游模型部署中的对抗迁移攻击。

Method: 提出SW-ProxyCE框架：通过收缩白化（shrinkage-whitening）处理从少量有标签参考集提取的类原型，恢复任务级决策几何结构，实现无需查询的、任务感知的对抗样本生成，且无需额外训练替代分类器。

Result: 在三个EEG任务上，使用三个通用基础编码器和一个范式特定预训练编码器进行评估，覆盖线性探测和全微调下游模型，以及跨被试和被试内场景。SW-ProxyCE生成的对抗样本能有效迁移到不可访问的下游模型，且一致优于任务无关的表示偏移攻击。

Conclusion: EEG基础模型的强迁移性并不必然带来对抗鲁棒性。公开编码器+私有下游模型的部署方式存在被对抗迁移攻击利用的实质性风险，需要引起重视。

Abstract: Electroencephalography (EEG) foundation models have recently emerged as a promising paradigm for EEG decoding by learning reusable representations from large-scale heterogeneous neural recordings. However, the open release of EEG foundation encoders, while facilitating downstream developments, also introduces a previously unexplored security risk: publicly available representations may make private downstream models vulnerable. This paper investigates adversarial transfer attacks in EEG foundation model deployment in a public-encoder and private-downstream setting, where attackers have white-box access to a released encoder and a small task-matched labeled reference set, but no access or query to victim parameters, outputs, or gradients. We propose Shrinkage-Whitened Proxy Cross-Entropy (SW-ProxyCE), a query-free task-aware attack framework that recovers task-level decision geometry from a small labeled reference set through shrinkage-whitened class prototypes, enabling transferable adversarial generation without training an additional surrogate classifier. We evaluated SW-ProxyCE across three EEG tasks using three general-purpose foundation encoders and a paradigm-specific pre-trained encoder, covering both linear-probing and full-fine-tuning downstream models in cross-subject and within-subject scenarios. Results demonstrated that adversarial examples generated from the public encoder and limited labeled references can effectively transfer to inaccessible downstream models. SW-ProxyCE consistently outperformed task-agnostic representation-shift attacks, revealing that the strong transferability of EEG foundation models does not necessarily lead to adversarial robustness. Our code will be available on GitHub.

</details>


### [163] [DOW-KE: Anchor-Free Multi-Layer Knowledge Editing via Direct End-to-End Weight Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16932)
*Ran Chen,Junbo Zhang,Qianli Zhou,Xinyang Deng,Wen Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DOW-KE是一种无锚点（anchor-free）的知识编辑方法，核心原则是"被优化的必须就是被部署的"。它通过完整模型的端到端反向传播，联合优化所有编辑层的权重更新，并将保留投影嵌入更新参数化中，消除了传统locate-then-edit方法中锚点目标与最终编辑效果之间的闭合差距。在两大数据集和三个模型上的大规模序列编辑实验中，六个模型-数据集组合中有五个在总体Score和邻域特异性上达到最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多层locate-then-edit方法先在选定层优化目标残差流激活（锚点），再逐层实现为权重更新。但这种方式优化的中间表示与最终部署的多层权重更新之间存在"闭合差距"（closure gap）——每次更新来自局部求解，传播导致的衰减和失真未被校正。

Method: DOW-KE采用无锚点设计，通过完整模型反向传播最终编辑目标，联合优化所有编辑层更新，使跨层传播和耦合进入每个梯度步骤。同时将保留投影嵌入更新参数化的计算图中，使每个梯度作用于实际部署的更新，优化过程中编辑自然避开受保护的知识。

Result: 在两个数据集和三个模型上的大规模序列编辑实验中，DOW-KE在六个模型-数据集设置中的五个取得了最高的总体Score和邻域特异性，优于所有评估基线。

Conclusion: DOW-KE验证了"优化与部署一致性"这一原则在知识编辑中的有效性：通过在计算图中联合优化权重更新并内嵌保留约束，可以系统性消除中间表示优化与实际部署之间的差距，提升编辑效果和知识保留能力。

Abstract: Multi-layer locate-then-edit methods for knowledge editing first optimize target residual-stream activations (anchors) at selected layers, then realize them layer by layer as weight updates. This pipeline optimizes an intermediate representation but deploys multi-layer weight updates whose joint effect through the true forward pass is never itself optimized: regardless of how anchors are set or propagated, each update comes from a local solve, so propagation-induced attenuation and distortion go uncorrected, leaving a closure gap between anchor targets and realized edits. We propose DOW-KE, an anchor-free method built on a single principle: what is optimized must be exactly what is deployed. DOW-KE backpropagates the final editing objective through the complete model, jointly optimizing the updates of all edited layers so cross-layer propagation and coupling enter every gradient step. The same principle dictates where preservation resides: embedding the preservation projection in the update parameterization, inside the computation graph, makes every gradient act on the deployed update; post-hoc constraints would reopen the gap, and the constrained search keeps edits clear of protected knowledge. In large-scale sequential editing on two datasets and three models, DOW-KE achieves the highest overall Score and neighborhood Specificity in five of six model-dataset settings among the evaluated baselines.

</details>


### [164] [Study-Strategy Clusters from EdNet Logs Track Engagement, Not Mastery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16963)
*Qingchuan Lyu,Yingxin Li,Albert Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在EdNet-KT3数据集上检验了"学习分析中无监督聚类ITS日志得到的用户类型应能预测学习效果"这一假设。通过聚类学习策略特征得到8种稳定的学习策略风格（父级5类+4种精细子风格），发现早期聚类能预测后期参与度（坚持性η²≈0.106，完成度η²≈0.021），但不能预测后期无辅助准确率（p_adj≈0.093）。知识追踪模型（SAKT）仅比基线略好（AUC提升+0.051），且掌握信号与行为风格几乎无关（ARI=0.007）。结论：行为聚类描述的是学习风格和参与度，而非知识收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习分析领域普遍假设从智能辅导系统（ITS）日志中聚类得到的"学习者类型"应该能够预测学习成效。作者旨在检验这一假设是否成立。

Method: 使用EdNet-KT3数据集中5,000名活跃学习者，对学习策略特征（资源使用、复习、视频、问题练习）进行聚类，选择轮廓系数确定k=5的父级聚类，再对残余大量（~64.9%）进行二次聚类得到4种精细风格；按响应数切分时间线，早期半段用于聚类、晚期半段用于结果评估；采用SAKT知识追踪模型对比基线，并用ARI评估指标对不同聚类与知识状态标签的一致性。

Result: 得到8种带自举稳定性的命名学习策略（如阅读导向、视频密集型、复习导向、问题优先等4个对比极及4种精细风格）。早期聚类显著预测后期参与度（坚持性η²≈0.106；完成度η²≈0.021），但无法预测无辅助准确率（p_adj≈0.093）。仅基于学习量的聚类与策略标签匹配度极低（ARI=0.064），行为聚类与知识掌握状态几乎独立（ARI=0.007）。SAKT模型预测能力仅比基线AUC提升+0.051。

Conclusion: 行为聚类反映的是学习风格与参与度模式，而非知识获得情况。研究警示：不能将ITS日志聚类自动等同于可预测学习效果的"学习者类型"。

Abstract: Learning analytics often treats unsupervised clusters of intelligent tutoring system (ITS) logs as learner types that should predict learning. We test that assumption on EdNet-KT3. Clustering study-strategy features (resource use, revision, video, problem practice) for 5{,}000 active learners yields a silhouette-selected parent cut ($k=5$) with 4 contrast poles (reading-focused, video-heavy, revision-heavy, and problem-first) plus a large near-mean residual ($\sim$64.9\%). Reclustering that residual adds four finer styles, giving a bootstrap-stable hierarchy of 8 named strategies. We split each learner's timeline by respond count so clusters use only the early half and outcomes only the late half. Early clusters predict later engagement (continuing to practice and finishing late sessions, especially persistence, $η^{2}\approx 0.106$; completion $η^{2}\approx 0.021$) but not later unassisted accuracy (correctness on late first-attempts without help; $p_{\mathrm{adj}}\approx 0.093$). Volume rises with some styles, yet volume-only clustering barely matches strategy labels (ARI$=0.064$). A knowledge-tracing model (SAKT) on the seven TOEIC exam sections predicts next correctness only modestly better than a baseline that knows only how hard each section usually is (AUC lift $+0.051$; CI $[+0.045,+0.058]$), and that mastery signal is nearly independent of behavior styles (ARI$=0.007$). Behavioral clustering here describes study styles and engagement, not knowledge gains.

</details>


### [165] [RoBell-RVFL: A Robust Generalized Bell Random Vector Functional Link Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16965)
*A. Rahaman,A. Quadir,M. Tanveer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RoBell-RVFL，一种鲁棒且轻量级的质量感知广义贝尔随机向量函数连接网络，通过双策略样本级加权机制应对类别不平衡和标签噪声问题。该机制对少数类样本使用单位权重严格保留信息，对多数类样本通过概率加权广义贝尔隶属函数在核诱导特征空间中自适应调节影响，有效抑制噪声、边界和离群样本。在UCI和KEEL基准数据集及高达40%标签噪声的鲁棒性测试中，RoBell-RVFL一致且显著优于最新的RVFL变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实数据集中的多数类主导问题会使随机神经网络产生决策边界偏差，忽略关键少数类样本。现有方法如SMOTE和类别加权损失函数主要处理类别比例而忽视类内分布，在标签噪声和离群值面前表现脆弱。需要一种能同时应对类别不平衡和噪声数据的鲁棒解决方案。

Method: 提出RoBell-RVFL，采用双策略样本级加权机制：(1) 少数类样本使用单位权重以严格保留信息；(2) 多数类样本通过概率加权广义贝尔(gbell)隶属函数在核诱导特征空间中自适应调节其影响力。该设计能有效抑制多数类中的噪声、边界和离群样本，同时将局部类别概率和类别分布信息显式融入学习过程，保持RVFL网络闭式学习的高效性。

Result: 在UCI和KEEL基准数据集上进行广泛评估，并在高达40%标签噪声条件下进行鲁棒性测试，RoBell-RVFL一致且显著优于最新的RVFL变体方法。实验结果表明自适应、质量感知的样本加权对鲁棒RVFL学习至关重要。

Conclusion: 自适应、质量感知的样本加权是鲁棒RVFL学习的关键要素，传统的全局加权方案在噪声和不平衡环境中效果不佳。RoBell-RVFL通过融合局部类别概率与类别分布信息，在保持闭式学习效率的同时实现了对样本贡献的自适应控制。

Abstract: The dominance of majority classes in real-world datasets poses a fundamental challenge to randomized neural networks, often biasing decision boundaries and overlooking critical minority samples. Existing remedies, such as synthetic minority over-sampling (SMOTE) and class-weighted loss functions, primarily address class proportions while neglecting intra-class distribution, making them vulnerable to label noise and outliers. In this paper, we propose \textbf{RoBell-RVFL}, a robust and lightweight \emph{quality-aware} generalized bell random vector functional link network that redefines how randomized models handle class imbalance and noisy data. RoBell-RVFL employs a dual-strategy, sample-level weighting mechanism that strictly preserves minority class information using unit weights, while adaptively regulating the influence of majority class samples through a probability-weighted generalized bell (gbell) membership function in a kernel-induced feature space. This design effectively suppresses noisy, boundary, and outlier samples within the majority class, enabling the network to learn from informative samples rather than merely abundant ones. By explicitly incorporating local class probability and class distribution information into the learning process, RoBell-RVFL achieves adaptive control over sample contributions without sacrificing the closed-form learning efficiency of RVFL networks. Extensive evaluations on UCI and KEEL benchmark datasets, along with robustness tests under up to 40\% label noise, demonstrate that RoBell-RVFL consistently and significantly outperforms recent state-of-the-art RVFL variants. The results indicate that adaptive, quality-aware sample weighting is essential for robust RVFL learning, rendering conventional global weighting schemes ineffective in noisy and imbalanced environments.

</details>


### [166] [MultiSigBERT: Beyond Survival Analysis through Multimodal and Sequential Modeling in Oncology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16972)
*Paul Minchella,Stéphane Chrétien,Guillaume Metzler,Loïc Verlingue,Rémi Vaucher*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MultiSigBERT，一种基于路径签名表示的多模态序列生存分析框架，将EHR中的自由文本报告与结构化变量联合建模，在里昂贝拉尔中心2500多名患者的肿瘤队列上达到0.743的一致性指数（C-index）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子健康记录（EHR）包含互补的异构数据（文本报告、数值测量、结构化变量），但现有生存模型大多局限于单一模态，或未能充分利用患者轨迹的时间特性，缺乏多模态时序联合建模的方法。

Method: MultiSigBERT将叙事性医学报告通过提取并平均上下文词嵌入转换为句子嵌入，经模态特定PCA压缩后与结构化协变量拼接形成联合时序轨迹；随后利用粗糙路径理论中的Signature变换高效编码跨模态的高阶时间交互（无需监督），最后将Signature特征作为高维特征输入LASSO正则化Cox模型进行风险评分估计。

Result: 在里昂贝拉尔中心的真实肿瘤队列（超过12万份医疗报告、2500余名患者）上进行验证，模型在独立测试集上实现了0.743（标准差0.029）的一致性指数。

Conclusion: 联合建模多模态时间动态与患者层面的几何结构，能够显著提升生存预测性能，验证了基于路径签名的统一框架在肿瘤学多模态序列生存建模中的有效性。

Abstract: Machine learning has become an essential component of modern healthcare, where the integration of heterogeneous data sources offers unprecedented opportunities to improve clinical decision-making.
