However, today's data-hungry algorithms make collecting sufficient demonstrations a struggle for tabletop manipulation, and even more so for humanoids that must also walk and balance. Learning from simulated data and transferring that behavior to the real world, as is commonly done in locomotion, sidesteps this struggle, so we replicate that recipe for loco-manipulation. In doing so, we find that cloning synthetic demonstrations results in a low performance ceiling no matter the amount of training data. Reinforcement learning breaks through it, and refining the cloned policy with Flow-GRPO on a single sparse reward yields performance that synthetic behavior cloning cannot match. Together, these stages form our end-to-end sim-to-real pipeline spanning more than 150,000 scenes, which we use to train FetchMan. We evaluate it on FetchMan-Bench, a simulation benchmark we release, and deploy it zero-shot on a real Unitree G1, where our single-object reach-and-pick policy walks to and grasps a target across unseen scenes at 73.3% success. Finally, we extend this recipe to multi-object training, a first step toward loco-manipulation generalist policies at this data scale.

</details>


### [238] [Lambda-Hold Control: Human-Like Movement Emerges from a Minimal Task Reward in Predictive Musculoskeletal Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17030)
*Jun Hyuk Lee,Chihyeong Lee,Jooeun Ahn*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出λ-hold控制器，受平衡点假说启发，以每块肌肉的平衡点阈值长度λ作为策略控制变量，并通过拉伸反射招募律自动计算肌肉兴奋度，同时将λ在各步态相位区间内保持恒定以减少策略查询频率。该控制器首次使肌肉驱动的骨骼模型仅用最小奖励即可在一小时内学会类人冲刺动作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类肌肉骨骼系统的过度驱动特性导致强化学习在探索高维且冗余的动作空间时效率极低，难以训练肌肉骨骼模型生成类人运动。

Method: 提出λ-hold控制器：1) 以每块肌肉的平衡点阈值长度λ作为策略的控制变量（受平衡点假说启发）；2) 通过拉伸反射招募律自动计算肌肉兴奋度；3) 在每个步态相位区间内保持λ恒定，大幅降低策略查询频率。

Result: 该控制器首次使肌肉驱动的骨骼模型仅需最小奖励即可在一小时内学会类人冲刺动作，实现了高效探索。

Conclusion: λ-hold控制器不仅是工程技巧，更是基于生理学的学习方法，融合了平衡点假说、间歇控制和最优反馈控制，有助于构建可学习的人类运动控制器模型。

Abstract: The massive overactuation in the human musculoskeletal system makes it challenging to train musculoskeletal models to generate human-like motion via reinforcement learning, primarily because exploration in the resulting high-dimensional and redundant action space is extremely inefficient. To address this problem, we propose the $λ$-hold controller, inspired by the equilibrium-point (EP) hypothesis, which has been widely supported by extensive evidence from human motor control studies. The policy's control variable is the per-muscle EP threshold length $λ$, from which a stretch-reflex recruitment law computes the muscle excitations automatically. Holding each $λ$ over an interval of the gait phase also sharply reduces the frequency at which the policy must be queried. Consequently, the controller, to our knowledge for the first time, enables a muscle-actuated skeletal model to learn human-like sprinting using only a minimal reward within an hour of training. The efficient exploration through the proposed $λ$-hold controller is not merely an engineering trick but an approach grounded in physiology, bringing together the EP hypothesis, intermittent control, and optimal feedback control. Beyond encapsulating human-like behavior in predictive simulation, this achievement contributes to developing a learnable model of the human motor controller.

</details>


### [239] [Terrain-Aware Local Path Planning with Global DEM Data Integration for Autonomous UGV Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17038)
*Devender Singh,Issah Nazif Suleiman,Paul Mitten,Glenn Cutler,Vinicius Prado da Fonseca,Matthew Hamilton*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种混合框架，将低分辨率DEM数据与实时LiDAR障碍物检测及地形分析相结合，用于无人地面车辆在复杂户外地形中的自主导航。通过基于DEM的A*全局路径规划配合局部传感器驱动的自适应路径修正，在Gazebo仿真中实现了95%的障碍物规避率，并将平均遭遇坡度从8°降至2.7°。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人地面车辆（UGV）在复杂户外地形中的自主导航面临关键挑战，其根本原因在于全局地图与实时传感器反馈之间存在固有的脱节。全局规划往往依赖于静态地图数据，难以应对户外环境中的动态变化和突发状况。

Method: 提出混合导航框架：首先利用预处理后的DEM数据通过A*算法计算全局路径；随后，基于局部LiDAR传感器数据驱动自适应路径修正，使UGV能够应对突发环境变化，在保证安全性的同时维持路径效率。

Result: 在Gazebo自定义地形仿真中，相比基线方法，该框架实现了95%的障碍物规避率，并将UGV平均遭遇坡度从8°显著降低至2.7°。

Conclusion: 该混合框架有效增强了路径效率和地形可通过性，支持鲁棒的实时自适应，为动态户外环境中更可靠的自主导航奠定了基础。

Abstract: Autonomous navigation in complex outdoor terrains presents critical challenges for unmanned ground vehicles (UGVs) due to the inherent disconnect between global mapping and real-time sensor feedback. This work proposes a hybrid framework that integrates low-resolution Digital Elevation Model (DEM) data with real-time LiDAR-based obstacle detection and terrain analysis for efficient path planning. A global path is initially computed using a preprocessed DEM-based A* algorithm. Subsequently, local sensor data drives adaptive path correction, enabling the UGV to negotiate sudden environmental changes while maintaining safety and efficiency. Simulation results in Gazebo demonstrate significant improvements over a baseline approach, achieving a 95\% obstacle avoidance rate and reducing the average encountered slope from $8^\circ$ to $2.7^\circ$ in custom terrain. This integration enhances path efficiency and terrain traversability and supports robust real-time adaptation, paving the way for more reliable autonomous navigation in dynamic outdoor environments.

</details>


### [240] [PDDL-ART: Autonomous Symbolic Abstraction From Demonstration For Long-Horizon Robotic Manipulation Using Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17146)
*Disha Kamale,Dmitry Berenson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PDDL-ART是一个基于视觉语言模型（VLM）的框架，能够从单个专家演示、自然语言任务描述和高层动作名称库中自主生成任务特定的PDDL领域与问题描述，无需领域模板或微调。通过多阶段（语法、语义、执行）修正管线，特别是利用VLM的工具调用能力进行符号谓词接地，在引擎维护和家庭场景的机器人操作任务中达到了93.3%的平均成功率，显著优于基线VLM规划器的78.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 符号规划（PDDL）为长时程机器人操作提供了理论框架，但构建准确的PDDL领域和问题描述需要大量领域专业知识，成为实际应用的主要瓶颈。现有的自动化方法通常依赖领域模板、动作签名或微调，缺乏通用性。

Method: PDDL-ART采用VLM从单一演示、自然语言描述和动作名库中生成PDDL描述，并引入多阶段修正管线（语法级、语义级、执行级）。在执行级修正中，利用VLM的工具调用能力进行符号谓词接地，通过几何和时间推理评估无法仅从图像中直接判别的关系谓词，模型自主决定何时调用工具及如何解释输出。

Result: 在引擎维护和家庭领域的高难度操作任务上（包括需要记忆、抽象谓词推理以及目标状态与初始状态视觉上难以区分的任务），PDDL-ART达到93.3%的平均成功率，而基线VLM规划器仅为78.3%。

Conclusion: PDDL-ART证明了利用现代VLM的工具调用能力可以大幅降低PDDL描述生成的门槛，无需领域模板或微调即可生成语义上对齐的符号规划描述，为机器人长时程操作中的符号规划自动化提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Symbolic planning with PDDL offers a principled framework for long-horizon robot manipulation, but constructing accurate PDDL domain and problem descriptions remains a significant bottleneck, typically requiring substantial domain expertise. We present a Vision-Language Model (VLM)-based approach called PDDL-ART, a framework that autonomously generates task-specific PDDL domain and problem descriptions from a single expert demonstration, a natural language task description, and a library of available high-level action names. PDDL-ART does not require any domain templates, action signatures, or fine-tuning. To ensure the generated descriptions are not only syntactically valid but semantically aligned with the demonstrated task, PDDL-ART introduces a multi-stage correction pipeline operating at syntactic, semantic, and execution levels. A key component of execution-guided correction is symbolic predicate grounding. Instead of relying solely on visual observations, PDDL-ART leverages the tool-use capabilities of modern VLMs to incorporate geometric and temporal reasoning for evaluating relational predicates that are not directly discernible from images alone. Critically, the model autonomously determines when to invoke these tools and how to interpret their outputs. We evaluate PDDL-ART on challenging manipulation tasks in engine maintenance and household domains, including tasks that require memory, abstract predicate inference, and goal states that are visually indistinguishable from the initial state. PDDL-ART achieves an average success rate of 93.3%, compared to 78.3% for a baseline VLM-based planner.

</details>


### [241] [Teach and Grow: An Agent-Centered Architecture for General Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17209)
*Chang Nie,Zhe Liu,Hesheng Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出"教与学成长学习"(TGL)架构，通过将少量成功示范转化为可复用的"技能块"并存储在技能库中，使机器人无需针对特定任务重新训练策略，即可在新场景中组合技能完成任务。文章还提出了"再训练税"概念及"教与学成长规模法则假设"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端视觉-语言-动作(VLA)与世界动作模型虽能实现通用机器人，但其可靠性受限于经验覆盖范围。当遇到陌生物体、传感器或情境时，需要新数据、策略更新和回归测试，这种反复的"再训练税"成为通用机器人学习的主要瓶颈，尤其是具身数据往往需要通过操作机器来创建。

Method: TGL是一个以智能体为中心的架构：1) 多模态智能体将少量成功示范转化为封闭循环的"技能块"(Skill Blocks)，用于实现有意义的子目标；2) 在新场景中，智能体锚定并组合这些技能块，选择学习或几何工具，观察物理结果，在执行偏离意图时修订路径；3) 技能库存储可执行行为，结构化经验记忆传递成功、失败和修复信息；4) 新任务无需特定任务的策略再训练。

Result: 在LIBERO基准评估中达到最先进性能；受控实验展示了技能归纳、持续复用和智能体引导的自适应能力。研究结果支持"教与学成长规模法则假设"：若X表示有效可重用经验，则未来任务误差和教学需求应随X的幂律趋近于不可约下限。

Conclusion: TGL将部署视为持续学习的过程，一个任务的完成可以降低下一个任务的难度。该架构提供了一种避免"再训练税"的途径，使通用机器人学习更接近真正的持续成长范式。

Abstract: End-to-end vision-language-action (VLA) and world-action models offer an elegant route to general-purpose robotics, but their reliability is bounded by validated physical coverage. When an unfamiliar object, sensor, embodiment, or contact falls outside that coverage and no validated fallback exists, correcting the failure requires new robot data, a policy update, and regression testing. This recurring burden is the retraining tax. Unlike text, embodied data must often be created by operating machines. We present Teach-and-Grow Learning (TGL), an agent-centered architecture for general robot learning. In its general form, a multimodal agent turns a few successful demonstrations into reusable Skill Blocks: closed-loop behaviors for meaningful subgoals. In a new scene, the agent grounds and composes these blocks, selects learned or geometric tools, observes the physical outcome, and revises the route when execution departs from intent. A Skill Library stores executable behavior, while structured Experience Memory carries forward success, failure, and repair. New tasks are acquired without task-specific policy retraining. Our LIBERO evaluation attains state-of-the-art performance; controlled studies expose skill induction, persistent reuse, and agent-directed adaptation. Finally, we propose the Teach-and-Grow scaling-law hypothesis: if X denotes effective reusable experience, future-task error and teaching demand should approach irreducible floors as power laws in X. The architecture therefore treats deployment as a period of continued learning, in which one task can make the next easier.

</details>


### [242] [Robust Brachiation on a Life-Sized Dual-Arm Robot Using Waypoint-Guided Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17320)
*Ayumu Iwata,Kento Kawaharazuka,Keita Yoneda,Takahiro Hattori,Kei Okada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于强化学习的方法，通过Waypoint-Guided Reinforcement Learning（WGRL）框架，在真实尺寸的双臂机器人上实现了稳健的臂行运动（brachiation），并在仿真和硬件实验中验证了包括失败恢复在内的鲁棒行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 臂行运动需要高度协调的全身运动和对抓握、释放的精确时序控制，在真实尺寸机器人平台上实现稳健行为极具挑战，现有的模仿学习方法由于缺少高难度任务的演示数据而难以应用。

Method: 提出了Waypoint-Guided Reinforcement Learning（WGRL）框架，通过稀疏指定末端执行器轨迹的路径点来引导行为习得，全身运动由强化学习生成；同时将路径点跟随引导与基于任务成功和机械能的奖励相结合，并在面向Sim-to-Real迁移的环境中训练。

Result: 在具有几何变化的猴杠环境下的Sim-to-Sim实验和硬件实验均验证了方法的有效性，确认了包括失败恢复行为在内的稳健臂行运动。

Conclusion: 本研究为在真实尺寸机器人硬件上实现基于手臂的 locomotion 提供了有效的学习设计指南，并扩展了机器人可穿越的工作空间。

Abstract: Brachiation is a form of locomotion in which primates move primarily using their arms, enabling traversal in environments without footholds. However, this motion requires highly coordinated whole-body movement and precise timing control for bar grasping and release. As a result, achieving robust behavior on life-sized robotic platforms remains challenging. In this study, we present a reinforcement learning-based method to realize brachiation on a life-sized dual-arm robot. The core of the proposed approach is Waypoint-Guided Reinforcement Learning (WGRL), a learning framework for inducing non-linear and complex motions. For high-difficulty tasks where imitation learning data are unavailable, WGRL guides behavior acquisition by sparsely specifying waypoints for the end-effector trajectory, while whole-body motion is generated through reinforcement learning. In addition, by integrating the waypoint-following guidance with rewards based on task success and mechanical energy, and training in an environment designed for Sim-to-Real transfer, the proposed method achieves both forward progression and motion stability. The acquired behavior is evaluated through Sim-to-Sim experiments under monkey-bar environments with geometric variations and hardware experiments, confirming robust brachiation including failure recovery behavior. This study provides effective learning design guidelines for realizing arm-based locomotion on life-sized robotic hardware and expanding the traversable workspace of robots.

</details>


### [243] [ORPA: Online Residual Policy Adaptation for Robot Manipulation Control with Human Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17323)
*Muhammad A. Muttaqien,Tomohiro Motoda,Ryo Hanai,Yukiyasu Domae*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了在线残差策略自适应（ORPA）框架，通过对预训练策略添加轻量级反馈条件模块，在关节空间预测残差调整，实现无需重新训练策略的实时动作修正，在ALOHA平台上的精密操作任务中表现出更高的成功率和更好的扰动恢复能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模仿学习策略（如ACT）对执行误差和分布偏移敏感，传统修正方法需要数据聚合和全策略重训练，计算成本高且不适合实时部署。

Method: ORPA框架：在不修改底层策略参数的前提下，为预训练控制策略附加一个轻量级的、以反馈为条件的模块，直接在关节空间预测残差调整，实现运行时自适应行为修正。

Result: 在ALOHA平台上针对多个精密敏感操作任务进行评估，相比基线控制策略和基于规则的运动学逆解修正方法，ORPA在成功率和从微小扰动中恢复的能力上均有提升。

Conclusion: ORPA为实时、轻量的机器人策略在线纠错提供了一种有效方案，避免了昂贵的重训练过程，提升了精密操作任务的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation policies trained via imitation learning, such as Action Chunking with Transformers (ACT), can achieve strong performance under ideal conditions but often remain sensitive to small execution errors and distribution shifts. Correcting these failures typically requires dataset aggregation and full-policy retraining, which is computationally expensive and unsuitable for real-time deployment. In this work, we propose Online Residual Policy Adaptation (ORPA), a framework that enables immediate, feedback-driven correction of robot actions without modifying the underlying policy parameters. ORPA augments a pretrained control policy with a lightweight, feedback-conditioned module that predicts residual adjustments directly in joint space, allowing the system to adapt its behavior at runtime. We evaluate ORPA on a set of precision-sensitive manipulation tasks using the ALOHA platform, demonstrating improvements in success rate and recovery from small perturbations compared to baseline control policies and rule-based inverse kinematics corrections.

</details>


### [244] [Reconfiguration-Complete Motion Primitives with Constructive Planning for Deformable Planar Modular Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17324)
*Jie Gu,Tingting Wang,Hongrun Gao,Yirun Sun,Zhihao Xia,Chunxu Tian,Dan Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种方形单元抽象方法，将可变形菱形模块映射为固定大小的网格单元，通过旋转和剪切两种原始运动实现可重构规划。证明了N≥7的非直线边连通配置均可变换为规范阶梯形，并基于此设计了阶梯规范化规划器及边界到投递的启发式选择器，实验验证了其在规划时间上的显著优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模块化机器人的连续可变形几何特性使得为其规划和分析定义一个固定的表示变得困难，这阻碍了可重构规划的高效实现。

Method: 提出方形单元抽象方法，将可变形菱形模块映射为固定大小网格单元，同时通过旋转（pivoting）和剪切（shearing）两种原始运动保留物理上可解释的局部运动。在此抽象下，构造性地证明任意非直线边连通配置（N≥7）可通过合法原始运动变换为规范阶梯形，并基于此构建阶梯规范化规划器，辅以边界到投递的启发式选择器来排序合法高层选择。

Result: 实验展示了构造性重构过程，启发式选择器显著降低了规划时间；与先前框架在共享模块数量上的对比表明，本文方法规划时间更低。

Conclusion: 由于原始运动可逆，任意两个满足条件的配置均可相互重构，该方法在保证完备性的同时有效提升了规划效率，为可变形模块化机器人的可重构规划提供了新的理论基础和实用算法。

Abstract: The continuously deformable geometry of modular robots makes it difficult to define a fixed representation for reconfiguration planning and analysis. This letter introduces a square-cell abstraction that maps deformable rhombus modules to fixed-size grid cells while retaining physically interpretable local motions through two primitives, pivoting and shearing. Under this abstraction, we prove that every non-straight edge-connected configuration with $N \geq 7$ can be transformed to a fixed canonical staircase using only admissible primitive motions. Since these motions are reversible, any two configurations in this class are mutually reconfigurable. The proof is constructive and directly yields a staircase-canonicalization planner that transports removable boundary modules while preserving connectivity. As a practical enhancement, we further introduce a boundary-to-delivery lookahead selector that ranks admissible high level choices without affecting the completeness guarantee. Experiments demonstrate the constructive reconfiguration process and show that the selector substantially reduces planning time, while reference comparisons indicate lower planning times than the prior framework over the shared module counts.

</details>


### [245] [MANIGUARD: A Benchmark and Data Suite for Specification-Grounded Safety Evaluation and Improvement of Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17386)
*Yiyan Peng,Philip Wang,Simon Sinong Zhan,Yiqi Lyu,Zhenyang Ni,Jixin Yan,Fiorelli Wong,Ruochen Jiao,Hang Yin,Xinyu Cao,Huajie Shao,Manling Li,Ruohan Zhang,Qi Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 ManiGuard，一个基于规范（specification）的框架，用于评估和提升基础模型机械臂操作策略的安全性。框架包含 ManiGuard-Bench 任务套件（6 类接触密集型家庭任务、200 个基础任务、1000 个锁定场景）和配套的安全标注轨迹生成管道（8000 条演示）。通过 LTL_f 自动机监控器进行物理基础的运行时安全检查，实验发现成功任务中有 6-21% 违反安全规范，微调后可提升安全任务完成率，但仍有显著安全差距无法通过增加数据量弥补。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管基础模型操作策略在任务成功率上进步迅速，但缺乏对它们是否安全成功的严格评估。现有评估过分关注任务成功而忽略安全性，且缺乏系统化的安全评估框架。

Method: 构建 ManiGuard-Bench 任务套件，按技能×约束分类法组织六类接触密集型家庭任务；每项任务在分布内和四种单轴分布外扰动下评估；使用 LTL_f 自动机监控器在物理基础谓词上运行时检查安全性（而非学习分类器或 LLM 判断）；配套管道将自动运动规划与人工遥操作结合，生成按步骤安全标注的 8000 条演示。

Result: 基准测试零样本和微调的 VLA 模型超过 23000 次rollout后：(i) 6-21% 的成功 rollout 违反安全规范；(ii) 微调后安全任务完成率从接近零提升到 7.5-29.8%，参与且安全的行为从 16-40% 提升到 51-72%；(iii) 仍有差距无法通过扩大演示规模弥补，21-42% 的参与 rollout 仍违反规范，六个任务族中有两个每个策略的安全成功率低于 2%，且失败在分布偏移和硬件上持续存在。

Conclusion: 安全的评估必须独立于任务成功进行，基础模型策略在安全性上仍有显著不足。ManiGuard 提供的规范驱动评估框架和微调数据虽能提升安全性，但单纯扩大数据规模无法完全弥合安全差距，需要更根本的安全保证方法。

Abstract: Foundation-model policies for robotic manipulation are advancing rapidly on task success, but rigorous evaluation of whether they succeed safely is still lacking. We introduce ManiGuard, a specification-grounded framework for evaluating and improving the safety of foundation-model manipulation, comprising the ManiGuard-Bench task suite and a paired safety-annotated trajectory-generation pipeline. ManiGuard-Bench organizes six contact-rich household task families into 200 locked base tasks along a skill $\times$ constraint taxonomy, with safety specified independently of task success. Each task is evaluated under one in-distribution and four single-axis out-of-distribution perturbations that hold the safety specification fixed, giving 1,000 locked scenarios. Every rollout is runtime-checked by LTL$_f$-grounded automaton monitors over physics-grounded predicates rather than learned classifiers or LLM judges, in simulation and on a physical Franka platform. The pipeline pairs an automated motion-planning generator with human teleoperation, annotated by the same per-step monitor, and directly supports safety-aware fine-tuning; we release 8,000 safety-annotated demonstrations, 40 per base task. Benchmarking zero-shot and fine-tuned VLAs across more than 23,000 rollouts, we find: (i) safety must be evaluated independently of task success, as 6-21% of successful rollouts violate the specification; (ii) fine-tuning on our suite raises safe task completion from near zero to 7.5-29.8% and engaged-and-safe behavior from 16-40% to 51-72%; but (iii) a gap remains that scaling demonstrations does not close, with 21-42% of engaged rollouts still violating, two of six families below 2% safe success for every policy, and these failures persisting under distribution shift and on hardware.

</details>


### [246] [Bi-Layer Ant Colony Optimization for Multi-Robot Task Allocation and Routing in Delivery Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17416)
*Le Na Nguyen,Thanh Long Nguyen,Thanh Thao Ton Nu,Quan Le,Manh Duong Phung*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种双层蚁群优化算法（Bi-layer ACO）解决多机器人任务分配（MRTA）问题，通过定义新的成本函数将任务分配与路径规划统一为优化问题，并在同一蚁群过程中集成两个相互依赖的决策层，实现同步优化。实验表明该方法在总行驶距离和完成时间上均优于MILP和PSO基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人任务分配（MRTA）在物流和配送应用中至关重要，但现有方法难以同时高效处理任务分配与路径规划的耦合优化问题，需要一种统一的优化框架来提升整体效率。

Method: 首先定义新的成本函数，将MRTA转化为同时涵盖任务分配与路径规划的统一优化问题；然后提出双层蚁群优化算法，在单一蚁群过程中集成两个相互依赖的决策层，形成层次化框架，实现任务分配与多机器人路径规划的同步优化。

Result: 与MILP和PSO对比实验显示，所提出的双层ACO在所有任务规模下均获得最短总行驶距离和最快完成时间；其中总行驶距离最多减少17.7%，完成时间最多缩短近20%。

Conclusion: 双层ACO算法在效率、可扩展性和可靠性方面均表现优异，适用于多机器人配送任务场景，验证了其作为MRTA解决方案的有效性。

Abstract: This paper addresses the multi-robot task allocation (MRTA) problem, which is essential for delivery and logistics applications. Our approach first defines a new cost function that transforms the MRTA into a unified optimization problem capturing both task assignment and routing. A bi-layer ant colony optimization (ACO) algorithm is then introduced, integrating two interdependent decision layers within a single colony process to solve the problem. This hierarchical framework enables simultaneous optimization of task allocation and route planning across multiple robots. Comparative experiments with mixed-integer linear programming (MILP) and particle swarm optimization (PSO) demonstrate that the proposed bi-layer ACO achieves the shortest total travel distance and fastest completion time across all task sizes. Specifically, it reduces total travel distance by up to 17.7% and completion time by nearly 20% compared with baseline methods. These results confirm the efficiency, scalability, and reliability of the proposed bi-layer ACO for multi-robot delivery tasks.

</details>


### [247] [Prism-GRPO: Faster VLA Policy Optimization via Splitting Same-outcome Groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17423)
*Zeyun Deng,Yuzhe Lu,Yawei Wang,Linbo Liu,Qing Ping,Han Ding,Guande Wu,Panpan Xu,Jun Huan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Prism-GRPO方法，通过将二值成功率奖励增强为带权重的轨迹级执行质量评分，解决了GRPO在VLA策略强化学习中因同结果分组产生零优势而被丢弃浪费采样预算的问题。该方法在四个RoboTwin任务中最高可减少56%的采样次数，并抑制奖励黑客行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO因无需训练critic而越来越多地被用于VLA策略的强化学习，但其依赖组间相对优势，需要多次采样。在二值成功奖励下，所有成功或所有失败的分组优势为零会被动态采样丢弃，尤其在训练早期大部分采样失败时浪费大量昂贵的机器人采样预算。

Method: Prism-GRPO将二值结果奖励与加权轨迹级执行质量评分相结合，将相同结果的分组拆分为质量谱系以恢复训练信号，同时保证任何成功都排在任何失败之上。质量分数可来自模拟器接触、执行动作或视觉观测，无需特定任务进度奖励。论文还证明了Prism-GRPO不会增加分组被丢弃（零优势）的概率，并推导了梯度对齐条件以确保组合更新保持任务成功的局部上升方向。

Result: 在四个涵盖不同任务时长和协调模式的RoboTwin任务中，Prism-GRPO在相同采样预算下提升了成功率和质量，并以最多56%更少的样本达到目标成功率。它还能抑制奖励黑客捷径，且更干净的行为可直接迁移到真实机器人部署。消融实验表明，接触、平滑度和VLM派生的质量信号均能带来一致的增益。

Conclusion: Prism-GRPO通过引入轨迹级质量评分有效恢复了GRPO在二值奖励下被丢弃的采样信号，在提升样本效率和任务成功率的同时抑制了奖励黑客行为，且不依赖任务特定的进度奖励，具有较好的泛化性和可部署性。

Abstract: GRPO is increasingly used for reinforcement learning of vision-language-action (VLA) policies because, unlike PPO, it does not require training a critic. This simplification comes with a sampling cost: group-relative advantages require multiple rollouts from each scene. Under binary success rewards, groups whose rollouts all succeed or all fail have zero advantage and are discarded by dynamic sampling. These groups are especially common early in training, when most rollouts fail, wasting much of the expensive robotic rollout budget. We introduce Prism-GRPO, which augments binary outcome reward with a weighted trajectory-level execution-quality score. By splitting same-outcome groups into a quality spectrum, Prism-GRPO recovers training signal while ensuring that every success still outranks every failure. Quality scores can be derived from simulator contacts, executed actions, or visual observations, avoiding task-specific progress rewards. We prove that Prism-GRPO never increases the probability that a sampled group is discarded for having zero advantages, and derive a gradient-alignment condition under which its combined update remains a local ascent direction for task success. Across four RoboTwin tasks spanning different horizons and coordination patterns, Prism-GRPO improves success and quality at matched rollout budgets and reaches target success rates with up to 56% fewer rollouts. It also suppresses a reward-hacking shortcut, with the cleaner behavior transferring under direct deployment to a real robot. Through ablations, we show consistent gains across contact-, smoothness-, and VLM-derived quality signals.

</details>


### [248] [UniReflex: Plug-and-Play Force Control for Pretrained Generative Policies via Fast-Slow Reflex](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17432)
*Yan Huang,Shoujie Li,Ziwu Song,Wenbo Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: UniReflex是一个通用即插即用框架，通过向冻结的生成式模仿学习策略注入可变阻抗控制，实现接触调节，无需重新训练骨干网络，在保持位置精度的同时显著提升接触稳定性，并将每步反向延迟降低25-66倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式模仿学习策略擅长轨迹规划但缺乏闭环力调节能力，而直接将力模态纳入网络往往需要重新设计或重训练网络，代价高昂且效率低下。

Method: 非侵入式地从动作头截获深层潜在表征，驱动一个快速反射网络；该网络解耦主动力施加与外部交互响应，预测归一化各向异性刚度方向用于定向柔顺分配；并集成自适应门控机制，实现位置主导规划与力主导执行之间的无缝切换。

Result: 在真实双臂实验中，UniReflex显著提升了接触稳定性和成功率，同时保持了原有的位置精度；相比联合训练策略，在评估的骨干网络上每步反向延迟降低了25-66倍。

Conclusion: UniReflex证明无需微调庞大骨干网络即可为现有生成式策略赋予闭环力调节能力，是一种高效、通用且可插拔的接触控制解决方案。

Abstract: Generative imitation learning policies excel at trajectory planning but lack closed-loop force regulation, while directly incorporating force modalities often requires redesigning or retraining the network. We present UniReflex, a universal plug-and-play framework that equips frozen generative policies with variable impedance control (VIC) for contact regulation, guided by force-direction intent collected during demonstration, without further slow-backbone fine-tuning. By non-invasively intercepting deep latent representations from the action head, UniReflex drives a fast reflex network that decouples active force exertion from external interaction response. This scheme predicts normalized anisotropic stiffness directions for directional compliance allocation. Furthermore, UniReflex integrates an adaptive gating mechanism that enables seamless transitions between position-dominant planning and force-dominant execution. Real-world bimanual experiments demonstrate that UniReflex significantly improves contact stability and success rates while preserving original position accuracy. Our approach achieves 25-66x lower per-step backward latency relative to joint training strategies on the evaluated backbones.

</details>


### [249] [EATR-Stereo: Embodiment-Aware Routing of Paired Stereo Evidence for Humanoid Vision-Language-Action Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17453)
*Songwei Wu,Rui Zhao,Fan Yang,Zhongqiang Nie,Zhiduo Jiang,Wandong Sun,Yuwei Li,Yang Liu,Hong Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出EATR-Stereo，一种具身感知的令牌路由框架，用于长时程人形机器人视觉-语言-动作（VLA）控制。该方法保留主视图令牌，通过查询同步辅助视图令牌序列构建主视图对齐的跨视图辅助令牌（CVATs），并结合身体分割的本体感觉编码器进行令牌级路由，在冻结预训练VLA模型的情况下选择性整合立体视觉证据，在33自由度人形机器人上实现了60%的全任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人形机器人VLA视觉接口要么丢弃互补的立体视觉证据，要么融合额外观测时未保持主视图的原生通路，也未使辅助信息适配机器人本体。这限制了长时程任务中立体视觉信息的有效利用。

Method: 1) 保留主视图（primary-view）令牌；2) 构造主视图对齐的跨视图辅助令牌（CVATs），通过查询同步的辅助视图令牌序列实现；3) 使用身体分割的本体感觉编码器，基于机器人构型历史对令牌级辅助信息使用进行条件化路由；4) 在冻结的预训练VLA模型上增强语言和主视图视觉上下文。

Result: 在33自由度人形机器人（37维本体感觉状态）上，评估了9种配置，执行超过100次"搜索-接近-抓取-放置-返回"任务。EATR-Stereo实现60.0%全任务成功率、100.0%抓取成功率、80.0%阶段成功率。在严重不对称遮挡下，恢复率从纯CVAT的30%提升至80%。

Conclusion: 选择性路由的配对立体视觉证据能够改善空间接地（spatial grounding），从而支持可靠的长时程人形机器人VLA控制。消融实验表明保留主视图令牌和结合跨视图辅助特征与结构化本体感觉路由均至关重要。

Abstract: Long-horizon humanoid vision--language--action (VLA) control with head-mounted stereo cameras requires visual interfaces that can exploit complementary views while maintaining compatibility with pretrained representations. Existing interfaces often discard complementary stereo evidence or fuse additional observations without preserving the native primary-view pathway and adapting auxiliary information to robot embodiment. We present EATR-Stereo, an embodiment-aware token-routing framework that retains primary-view tokens and constructs primary-aligned Cross-View Auxiliary Tokens (CVATs) by querying the synchronized auxiliary-view token sequence. A body-segmented proprioceptive encoder further conditions token-wise auxiliary usage on robot configuration history, enabling selective incorporation of stereo evidence during action generation. The routed auxiliary stream augments the language and primary-visual context of a pretrained VLA while keeping its vision--language model frozen. On a 33-DoF physical humanoid with a 37-D proprioceptive state, we evaluate nine configurations in over-100-s search--approach--grasp--place--return tasks. EATR-Stereo achieves 60.0% full-task success, 100.0% grasp success, and 80.0% stage success. Under severe asymmetric occlusion, it improves recovery to 80% compared with 30% for CVAT alone. Ablation studies further show the importance of preserving primary tokens and combining cross-view auxiliary features with structured proprioceptive routing. These results demonstrate that selectively routed paired stereo evidence improves spatial grounding for reliable long-horizon humanoid VLA control.

</details>


### [250] [Reuse Before You Retrieve: Diagnosing Headroom and Complementarity for Test-Time Augmentation of Embodied Multimodal Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17484)
*Yuhwan Jeong,Kuk-Jin Yoon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了冻结的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略在测试时的干预选择问题，提出通过"可恢复余量"和"检索互补性"两个可测量因子来决定应采用额外采样还是外部演示检索，并在多个VLA骨干网络和环境中验证了选择器的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冻结VLA策略在测试时可通过采样额外行为或引入外部演示来提升性能，但缺乏明确指导来决定具体需要哪种干预方式。额外采样仅在策略随机轨迹中已存在可识别的好行为时有价值，而检索仅在策略未能可靠表示相关动作先验时最有效，因此需要系统化的决策框架。

Method: 提出两个可测量的关键因子：可恢复余量（recoverable headroom）表征策略中已有可恢复的有用行为量，检索互补性（retrieval complementarity）表征外部动作先验能否填补可测量的缺口。基于这两个因子设计了一个情节级别的重试选择器（episode-level retry selector），支持可重试或并行执行模式，并可与检索结合使用。

Result: 选择器在LIBERO上所有测试的VLA骨干网络中一致地恢复了大量潜在能力，成功率提升最高达21.0个百分点，且与可恢复余量紧密相关。该方法能迁移到不同机器人和模拟器，在观测退化条件下依然有效。自回归OpenVLA实验展示了可用余量与候选轨迹排序能力之间的区别。检索在具有最大动作先验差距的策略上效果最好，与选择结合可进一步增益。

Conclusion: 实验结果提供了表征测试时增强机会的经验基础，通过将可从冻结策略恢复的能力与可能需要外部引入的行为先验区分开，为部署VLA策略时选择何种测试时干预提供了实证依据。

Abstract: Frozen vision-language-action (VLA) policies are increasingly improved at test time by sampling additional policy behaviors or introducing external demonstrations. Yet there is little guidance for deciding which intervention a deployed policy actually needs. Additional sampling is useful only when better behavior already exists within the policy's stochastic rollouts and can be identified, whereas retrieval is most useful when the relevant action prior is not reliably represented by the policy. We study this decision through two measurable factors, recoverable headroom and retrieval complementarity, which characterize how much useful behavior is already available to recover and whether an external action prior fills a measurable gap. We evaluate an episode-level retry selector under retryable or parallel execution, together with retrieval across multiple frozen VLA policies and environments. The selector consistently recovers substantial latent capability across all tested VLA backbones on LIBERO, with gains of up to 21.0 success-rate points that closely track recoverable headroom. It also transfers to a different robot and simulator and remains effective under degraded observations, while experiments with autoregressive OpenVLA illustrate the distinction between available headroom and the ability to rank candidate rollouts. Retrieval behaves differently, improving the policy with the largest measured action-prior gap and providing further gains when combined with selection. Together, these results provide an empirical basis for characterizing test-time augmentation opportunities by separating capability that can be recovered from the frozen policy from behavioral priors that may need to be introduced externally.

</details>


### [251] [Calibrated Predictive Safety for Heterogeneous Robots: An Action-Conditioned JEPA Framework with Model-Based Safety Shields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17496)
*Kaiming Zhong,Tianhua Liu,Yue Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种重规划水平决策管线，将行动条件化JEPA世界模型与确定性安全防护罩结合，在异构机器人上实现既泛化又满足执行期保证的策略。系统通过候选动作块的前瞻预测（任务进度+物理风险）进行重排序，但最终合规性由确定性防护罩和回退阶梯保障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言-动作策略泛化能力强但缺乏执行期保证；经典基于模型规划器虽满足运动学和几何约束却泛化差。作者希望弥合两者鸿沟，构建既能像VLA泛化、又能提供执行期安全保证的可部署管线。

Method: 提出五步重规划决策管线：(1)候选动作块生成器产生K个候选块；(2)动作条件化JEPA在冻结编码器隐空间中对每个候选进行前向展开，并以实体嵌入为条件；(3)校准的风险与进度头对各展开结果评分并报告不确定性；(4)确定性逐实体安全防护罩过滤不可行候选；(5)空可行集时使用回退阶梯。学习排序仅重排可行候选，保证强制执行来自确定性防护罩与回退阶梯。

Result: 在LIBERO-Long仿真中的600回合配置下，完整框架相比仅防护罩基线提升了成功率，并在匹配召回率下减少了碰撞假阴性。报告了目标机器人与边缘加速器上的部署效率测量结果。

Conclusion: 该方法展示了JEPA世界模型与确定性安全机制结合的有效性，但真实机器人实验和离线重排序显著性检验尚属未来工作。

Abstract: Vision-language-action policies generalize broadly but provide no execution-time guarantees; classical model-based planners respect kinematic and geometric constraints but generalize poorly. We study whether an action-conditioned Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture (JEPA) world model can predict, before execution, both task progress and physical risk for candidate action chunks, and whether coupling these predictions to an embodiment-specific model-based safety shield yields a deployable pipeline for heterogeneous robots.
  We propose a receding-horizon decision pipeline: (1) a proposer produces K candidate action chunks; (2) an action-conditioned JEPA rolls each candidate forward in a frozen-encoder latent space conditioned on an embodiment embedding; (3) calibrated risk and progress heads score each rollout and report uncertainty; (4) a deterministic per-embodiment safety shield filters inadmissible candidates; (5) a fallback ladder handles empty-admissible-set cases. The learned ranking only reorders admissible candidates; enforcement guarantees come from the deterministic shield and fallback ladder.
  We evaluate with a pre-registered protocol in simulation (LIBERO-Long). In 600-episode configurations the full framework improved success over a shield-only baseline and reduced collision false negatives at matched recall. Deployment-efficiency measurements on target on-robot and edge accelerators are included. Real-robot experiments and an offline reranking significance test remain future work; see the paper for disclosures.

</details>


### [252] [Embodied-Navigator: Point, Think, Memorize, and Align for Efficient Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17512)
*Hongyan Feng,Sunlai Chen,Xuanyu Liu,Miao Pan,Yangfan Xie,Yuxiang Cui,Zhongxiang Zhou,Rong Xiong,Wenqi Zhang,Jianwei Yin,Yueting Zhuang,Xuhong Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出TAMP-Nav框架，通过将导航任务重构成2D视觉提示（Pixel-to-3D）、引入选择性推理与锚点轨迹记忆机制、以及基于GRPO的两级对齐范式，实现高效具身导航，在R2R-CE上达到66.2%的成功率且仅需90k训练轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将大型视觉语言模型（VLM）强行嵌入不自然的动作空间，与其2D预训练先验不一致，同时存在僵化的推理调度和低效的记忆管理等问题，导致VLM在具身导航中的直接部署面临挑战。

Method: TAMP-Nav框架包含三个核心组件：(1) Pixel-to-3D Action Formulation (Point)：将导航重构为2D视觉提示，VLM仅选择2D像素并投影到3D坐标供SLAM控制器执行；(2) Selective Reasoning and Anchor-Trajectory Memory (Think and Memorize)：动态触发思维链推理，仅在关键节点保留高保真记忆，将冗余轨迹压缩为轻量级时空指示器；(3) Two-Level Alignment Paradigm (Align)：通过GRPO叠加全局结果奖励与细粒度过程奖励，实现密集监督对齐认知规划与环境反馈。

Result: 实验表明TAMP-Nav在R2R-CE基准上达到66.2%的SR（状态最优），且具有较高的运行时和样本效率，仅需90k条训练轨迹。

Conclusion: 通过将VLM的2D视觉先验与具身执行对齐、动态调度推理并优化记忆管理，TAMP-Nav验证了高效利用VLM进行具身导航的可行路径，为后续研究提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Although Large Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have significantly advanced embodied navigation, their direct deployment remains challenging, as existing methods often force VLMs into unnatural action spaces that misalign with their 2D pre-training priors, compounded by rigid reasoning schedules and inefficient memory management. To overcome these limitations, we propose TAMP-Nav, a unified framework for efficient embodied navigation. First, we introduce a Pixel-to-3D Action Formulation (Point) that reformulates navigation into 2D visual prompting. Specifically, the VLM merely selects 2D pixels, which are then projected into 3D coordinates for a low-level SLAM controller. This design naturally aligns embodied execution with the VLM's inherent 2D visual capabilities. Second, we propose an integrated Selective Reasoning and Anchor-Trajectory Memory mechanism (Think and Memorize), which dynamically triggers Chain-of-Thought and retains high-fidelity memory only at critical nodes, compressing redundant trajectories into lightweight Space-Time Indicators, thereby preserving critical historical information and enhancing spatio-temporal perception. Finally, we design an efficient Two-Level Alignment Paradigm (Align) via Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO). By superimposing global outcome rewards with fine-grained process rewards, this dense supervision tightly aligns the agent's cognitive planning with physical environmental feedback, endowing the model with adaptive reasoning capabilities. Experiments demonstrate that TAMP-Nav achieves state-of-the-art performance (e.g., 66.2% SR on R2R-CE) with high runtime and sample efficiency (requiring only 90k training trajectories).

</details>


### [253] [Scalix: Uncertainty-Aware Scale-Consistent Monocular SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17553)
*Sebastian Barbas Laina,Tianyi Zhang,Panagiotis Petropoulakis,Simon Schaefer,Simon Boche,Jaehyung Jung,Cedric Le Gentil,Stefan Leutenegger*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Scalix，一个实时单目SLAM框架，通过将学习到的深度线索（含每像素深度不确定性和每帧尺度不确定性）集成到概率因子图优化中，实现度量尺度的状态估计。该方法在户外和室内大规模环境中均取得了最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单目SLAM存在固有的尺度模糊问题，常用的视觉-惯性方案在机器人匀速运动时尺度不可观。虽然深度学习方法提供了新的解决思路，但现有学习到的深度图噪声大且跨帧尺度不一致，难以直接用于精确的状态估计。

Method: Scalix将学习到的深度线索集成到概率因子图框架中，具体包括：1）为现有单目深度模型补充每像素深度不确定性和每帧尺度不确定性；2）将尺度预测作为优化中的独立测量处理；3）通过多视图数据关联提升尺度一致性。

Result: 在大规模室外和室内环境的实验中，Scalix在度量尺度和归一化尺度两类基准上均取得了最先进的性能，同时保持了实时运行能力并展现良好的泛化性。

Conclusion: Scalix成功证明了将深度学习深度先验以不确定性量化的方式集成到因子图优化中，能够有效解决单目SLAM的尺度歧义问题，在保持实时性的同时实现了度量尺度的精确估计。

Abstract: Cameras are ubiquitous sensors in robotics due to their compact form factor and the perceptual richness captured through visual information. Monocular SLAM enables robots to understand the environment with a minimum setup, however, it inherently suffers from scale ambiguity. A common solution is to provide multi-modal sensor configurations, such as visual-inertial systems, where scale is observable unless the robot navigates under a constant-velocity motion, a common scenario in mobile robotics. With the advent of deep-learning, geometric foundation models have been used to address this problem, but the depths maps are often noisy and scale-inconsistent across frames. In this paper, we propose Scalix, a real-time monocular SLAM framework that achieves metric-scale state estimation by integrating learned depth cues into a probabilistic factor-graph formulation. By augmenting existing monocular depth models with both per-pixel depth uncertainty and per-frame scale uncertainty, Scalix treats scale predictions as independent measurements within its optimization, leading to improved scale consistency through multi-view data associations. Experiments in large-scale outdoor and indoor environments demonstrate state-of-the-art performance on both metric and up-to-scale benchmarks while maintaining real-time operation and generalization.

</details>


### [254] [HODAgent: Towards On-Demand, Responsive Humanoids for Physical World Human Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17584)
*Wang Warren Chen,Jiahao Zhang,Zhenjiang Li,Mingxu Wang,Lei Yi,Yuchen Kang,Shuo Sun,Ziping Chen,Jie Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出HODAgent，一个面向服务场景人形机器人的System-2具身智能体，采用半双工架构整合Env-Interactor、Planner、Executor和分层记忆模块，实现情境意图理解、响应执行、任务修订和结果验证。在164个交互仿真案例中达到84.8%和91.5%的联合成功率，物理机器人任务通过率63.3%-92%，并在多个具身基准上超越基线0.7-9.0个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 服务型人形机器人需要具备系统2级别的认知能力，以应对动态交互中的情境意图理解、执行过程中的响应调整、任务修订以及执行结果的验证等复杂挑战，现有方法在这些方面尚不完善。

Method: 提出半双工架构的系统2具身智能体HODAgent，包含Env-Interactor（环境交互）、Planner（规划）、Executor（执行）和分层记忆（Memory）模块，通过共享接口连接仿真与物理机器人（Unitree G1），隔离平台特定的控制逻辑。

Result: 在164个交互仿真案例中，基于两种VLM骨干网络分别达到84.8%和91.5%的联合成功率，比基线高出9.8和18.9个百分点；物理机器人上原子任务通过率92%、复合任务72%、完整任务63.3%；在多个具身基准上相比基线提升0.7-9.0个百分点。

Conclusion: 统一的多模态大语言模型驱动的System-2智能体架构能够使服务型人形机器人在仿真和真实环境中实现自适应服务能力，验证了该方案的有效性与泛化性。

Abstract: We propose HODAgent, a System-2 embodied agent for humanoid robots in service settings, addressing situated intent, responsive execution, task revision, and outcome verification. Its semi-duplex architecture integrates an Env-Interactor, Planner, Executor, and hierarchical Memory to maintain coherent interaction, planning, and task state during service episodes. This allows handling new requests during motion, retaining progress, revising actions, and grounding closure in execution outcomes. A shared interface connects simulation and physical robots (Unitree G1), isolating platform-specific control. In an interactive simulation with 164 cases, HODAgent achieves 84.8% and 91.5% Joint Success under two VLM backbones, outperforming baselines by 9.8 and 18.9 points. On physical robots, pass rates are 92% (atomic), 72% (composite), and 63.3% (complete tasks). On multiple embodied benchmarks, it improves over baselines by 0.7-9.0 points. Results show a unified System-2 agent enables adaptive humanoid service across simulation and reality.

</details>


### [255] [tinyDSM: A Framework for Skill Modeling and Development for Resource-Constrained Millirobots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17596)
*Markus D. Kobelrausch,Michael Miedler,Axel Jantsch*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究小型资源受限系统（如厘米级毫机器人）如何在寿命周期内自主探索、学习和适应能力。提出了tinyDSM框架，整合内在动机与适应度评估，以最小先验知识和硬编码技能为基础，通过强化学习实现开放式技能发展。物理实验中，一个36 cm³的毫机器人（Raspberry Pi Pico RP2040微控制器，9 kB内存）在15分钟内从基础运动技能自主进阶到复杂几何图案；仿真分析支持跨学习算法和内在动机参数的系统比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索资源极度受限的小型系统如何实现自主的终身学习与能力适应。传统方法依赖大量先验知识和充足计算资源，而厘米级毫机器人仅有极小的存储与算力，需要从通用、最小的起始知识出发，通过发育机制实现开放式技能习得。

Method: 提出tinyDSM框架，结合（a）内在动机驱动的发育机制，（b）认知架构（知识、推理、学习），（c）最小资源利用。使用层次化知识图谱和运动学推理器建模并评估简单至高级的运动技能。编码最少的先验通用知识，通过强化学习引导技能获取，并在物理机器人和仿真环境中进行验证比较。

Result: 36 cm³毫机器人在仅9 kB内存的RP2040微控制器上集成了全部功能（除摄像头外），15分钟内从线性/角度运动等基本动作自主学成复杂几何图案。仿真分析实现了跨学习算法和内在动机参数的系统性比较。

Conclusion: 最小先验知识与内在动机驱动的发育机制相结合，能在极度资源受限的毫机器人上实现有效的自主技能学习和开放式能力发展，为小型嵌入式系统的终身学习提供了可行方案。

Abstract: In this study, we investigate developmental mechanisms that enable small, resource-constrained systems such as cm-sized millirobots to autonomously explore, learn, and adapt their capabilities throughout their lifespan. Reinforcement learning algorithms guide the agent's skill acquisition and adaptation through the interplay of our proposed tinyDSM, which integrates intrinsic motivation and fitness-based assessment. We strive for minimal, hard-wired skills while encouraging the open-ended development of new skills. A key emphasis in our approach is to encode minimal a-priori general knowledge, which serves as a foundational starting point for the system as it further learns system-specific dependencies from the initial knowledge provided. Thus, by design, our approach attempts to cover very generic application domains. The methodology is based on (a) developmental mechanism with intrinsic motivation, and (b) a cognitive architecture (knowledge, reasoning, learning), while (c) utilizing minimal resources. It uses a hierarchical knowledge graph and kinematic reasoners to model and evaluate simple and advanced motion related skills. In our experiments, we use a resource-constrained millirobot with a volume of 36 cm^3 with a Raspberry Pi Pico 32-bit microcontroller (RP2040) that integrates all described features and capabilities except the camera system in 9 kB. Starting with learning the most elementary motor skills the millirobot autonomously progresses from simple linear and angular movements to complex geometric patterns within 15 minutes. To complement the physical experiments, we perform a simulation-based analysis that enables systematic comparisons across learning algorithms and intrinsic motivation parameters.

</details>


### [256] [LIBERO-VIFO: Benchmarking the Capability and Safety of Visual Cue Following in Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17600)
*Zhengyan Qian,Rui Yan,Alex Jinpeng Wang,Jinhui Tang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出LIBERO-VIFO基准，系统评估VLA模型在视觉线索跟随上的能力与安全性，涵盖八种视觉线索族和四类协议，发现当前VLA模型虽不完全理解线索但已能执行线索指任务，暴露了未经授权视觉线索跟随的安全风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作对视觉线索形式覆盖狭窄，仅关注最终任务成功率，评估粗粒度；且将所有视觉线索视为已授权，忽略了未经授权跟随的安全风险，缺乏对VLA模型视觉线索跟随能力与安全性的系统评测。

Method: 构建LIBERO-VIFO基准，定义八种视觉线索族覆盖多样形式；设定两大部分共四个协议：第一部分测试线索理解与授权跟随，第二部分在语言线索冲突和空语言条件下评估未经授权的视觉线索跟随；对七个VLA模型进行评测，并扩展场景实例化线索、安全关键设置及真实机器人部署实验。

Result: 评测发现视觉线索理解并不可靠地转化为执行能力；当前VLA模型能够在无语言指令的情况下执行线索指示的任务，暴露出未经授权视觉线索跟随的新兴风险；扩展实验进一步验证了这些发现。

Conclusion: LIBERO-VIFO将视觉线索跟随的能力与安全性纳入系统评估，为VLA社区建立了以视觉为中心的安全新视角。

Abstract: Visual cues are increasingly adopted to guide robot learning, but whether Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models can reliably follow authorized cues while disregarding unauthorized ones remains unclear. Existing work covers only a narrow range of cue forms and focuses on final task success, providing only a coarse assessment of cue-following capability. Treating all visual cues as authorized also leaves safety risks of unauthorized following unexplored. To address these gaps, we introduce LIBERO-VIFO, a benchmark to evaluate both the capability and safety of visual cue following in VLA models. LIBERO-VIFO defines eight visual cue families spanning diverse forms. A total of four protocols in two parts are defined: Part I tests cue understanding and authorized following, while Part II evaluates unauthorized visual cue following under language-cue conflict and empty language conditions. Evaluating seven VLA models reveals that although visual cue understanding does not reliably translate into execution, current VLAs are able to execute cue-indicated tasks without language instruction, exposing an emerging risk of unauthorized visual cue following. Extended experiments on scene-instantiated cues, safety-critical settings, and real-robot deployment corroborate these findings. LIBERO-VIFO brings both the capability and safety of visual cue following into systematic evaluation, establishing visual-centric safety as a new perspective for the VLA community.

</details>


### [257] [Physics-Informed Sliding-Window Particle Filtering for Tactile-Only In-Hand 6-DoF Object Pose Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17601)
*Lingjun Shao,Ying Zhang,Xiangfei Li,Xiangyang Li,Huan Zhao,Zhenyu Wang,Han Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究无视觉或视觉严重遮挡场景下，抓取物体的仅触觉6自由度位姿细化与信念维持问题，提出一种基于物理信息的SE(3)粒子滤波方法，融合主动接触符号距离一致性、法向对齐、摩擦锥可行性、零力负证据等多种似然项，并通过滑动窗口时序融合和势场引导采样提升位姿估计精度与多模态保持能力。在Allegro Hand V5上的五物体实验中，该方法在归一化ADD-S指标上优于多种基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在静态和短准静态手持操作中，视觉信息常常不可用或被严重遮挡，此时仅依靠触觉进行6自由度位姿估计十分关键。但触觉存在部分可观测性问题：全手触觉接触稀疏、间断且存在歧义（受限于有限的激励和物体对称性），使得位姿信念维持与细化成为一项挑战。

Method: 提出一种物理信息驱动的SE(3)粒子滤波方法。似然函数结合多类信息：主动接触符号距离一致性、力-法向对齐、摩擦锥可行性、零力负证据及可选可行性保护。采用滑动窗口对数似然融合多帧触觉数据以缓解单帧歧义；势场引导的提议分布使粒子远离手-物穿透区域；对称感知重采样保留多个合理的模态。

Result: 在Allegro Hand V5上对五个物体进行的实验中，该方法在归一化ADD-S指标上低于仅触觉的几何方法、粒子滤波基线和学习方法基线；消融实验证实了时序融合、势场引导和模态保持各自带来的增益。

Conclusion: 物理信息引导的粒子滤波结合时序融合与对称感知重采样，能够有效应对触觉部分可观测性带来的位姿歧义，是在无视觉条件下进行抓取物体6自由度位姿跟踪的可行方案。

Abstract: This paper studies tactile-only 6-DoF pose refinement and belief maintenance for grasped objects in static and short quasi-static in-hand configurations where vision is unavailable or heavily occluded. The key difficulty is tactile partial observability: whole-hand taxel contacts are sparse, intermittent, and ambiguous under limited excitation and object symmetries. We propose a physics-informed particle filter on $\mathrm{SE}(3)$ that updates pose beliefs from dense whole-hand tactile measurements. The likelihood combines active-contact signed-distance consistency, force-normal alignment, friction-cone feasibility, zero-force negative evidence, and optional feasibility guards. A sliding-window log-likelihood fuses recent tactile frames to reduce single-frame ambiguity, while a potential-field-guided proposal steers particles away from hand--object penetration. Symmetry-aware resampling preserves multiple plausible modes. Experiments on an Allegro Hand V5 with five objects show lower normalized ADD-S than tactile-only geometric, particle-filter, and learning baselines, and ablations confirm the benefits of temporal fusion, potential guidance, and mode preservation.

</details>


### [258] [Iterative Grasp Pose Refinement: A Deep Reinforcement Learning Approach for 2D Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17628)
*Amir Arsalan Nematollahi,Shayan Ahmadi,Mehdi Tale Masouleh,Ahmad Kalhor*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于强化学习的机器人抓取细化框架，融合关键点物体表征与深度Q网络（DQN），利用几何算法生成初始抓取候选并迭代优化，在Dex-Net数据集的300个物体上达到100%成功率，并通过物理实验验证了sim-to-real的迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发能够理解和操作物体的机器人需要紧凑、可解释且可泛化的表征。几何方法生成初始抓取候选存在失败情况，需要一种方法来将失败的抓取转化为成功的抓取，提升机器人在接触丰富操作任务中的抓取能力。

Method: 提出一种基于强化学习的抓取细化框架，包含三个核心要素：(1) 使用关键点物体表征提供紧凑且可解释的物体描述；(2) 采用深度Q网络(DQN)作为强化学习核心算法；(3) 在模拟环境中利用2D俯视图像，结合几何算法生成初始抓取候选，通过框架迭代细化，将失败抓取逐步转化为成功抓取。

Result: 在Dex-Net数据集300个物体上使用UR5机械臂进行实验，框架对几何方法认定不可抓取的物体实现了100%的成功率；通过Delta并联机器人的物理实验进一步验证了框架的sim-to-real迁移能力，成功操作了此前不可抓取的物体。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了强化学习在解决机器人抓取挑战中的有效性，特别是将关键点表征与DQN结合的方式，为接触丰富的操作任务提供了一种可扩展且适应性强的解决方案。

Abstract: Developing robots capable of understanding and manipulating objects requires compact, interpretable, and generalizable representations. This work proposes a reinforcement learning-based framework for robotic grasp refinement, integrating keypoint-based object representations with a Deep Q-Network (DQN). Using 2D overhead images captured in a simulated environment, a geometric-based algorithm generates initial grasp candidates, which are iteratively refined by the proposed framework, transforming failed grasps into successful ones. Experiments conducted on 300 objects from the Dex-Net dataset using a UR5 manipulator demonstrate the framework's effectiveness, achieving a 100% success rate on objects previously deemed ungraspable by geometrical methods. The framework's sim-to-real transferability is further validated through physical experiments on a Delta parallel robot, where a refined grasp successfully manipulates an object that was previously ungraspable. The findings underscore the effectiveness of reinforcement learning in addressing challenges in robotic grasping, offering a scalable and adaptable solution for contact-rich manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [259] [OVIP-SG: Open-Vocabulary Instance-Preserving Scene Graphs for Mapping and Retrieval of Small, Fine-Grained Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17633)
*Tianjing Hao,Haiyu Lan,Angsong Li,Cheng Chen,Enyu Li,Jiarui Yang,Yuning Su,Peiwen Lin,Wang Chuang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出OVIP-SG，一个统一的开放词汇3D场景图框架，用于实例保持的语义映射、功能场景分区和语言引导的小型细粒度物体检索。通过对称3D IoU关联和面积加权特征融合保留小物体实例，利用VLM推理物体功能进行场景分区，并采用四阶段级联检索流水线处理未知目标和目标缺失判断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放词汇感知在为3D场景图带来长尾类别和小物体检测能力的同时，也会导致大型表面碎片化和小型物体被合并到邻近大物体中，破坏实例级一致性。此外，现有方法难以检索未映射的目标或判断查询物体是否存在，阻碍了具身开放世界导航与探索。

Method: OVIP-SG采用VLM列举场景特定类别以进行鲁棒的开放世界检测；使用对称3D IoU关联和面积加权特征融合来保持小型独立实例；通过VLM推断物体功能将场景划分为紧凑的功能搜索区域；四阶段级联检索流水线结合体素投票并基于勘探覆盖率判断目标缺失。

Result: 在Replica统一评估协议下，OVIP-SG相比ConceptGraphs在mAcc上提升6.31点，F-mIoU提升5.15点，类无关原生实例PQ达0.398；搜索区域缩小至室内面积的21.8%，物体存在性分类平衡精度达0.773。真实机器人实验验证了其实用性。

Conclusion: OVIP-SG有效解决了开放词汇感知在3D场景图中导致的实例碎片化和合并问题，同时实现了高效的小物体检索和可靠的目标缺失判断，为具身开放世界导航提供了实用且有效的统一框架。

Abstract: Integrating open-vocabulary perception into object-level 3D scene graphs is a double-edged sword. While vision-language detectors recover long-tail categories and small, fine-grained objects overlooked by closed-set models, they also tend to fragment large surfaces and merge small objects into larger neighboring objects, compromising instance-level consistency and undermining mapping fidelity. Moreover, existing methods struggle to retrieve previously unmapped targets or determine whether a queried object is absent, hindering robust embodied open-world navigation and exploration. We present OVIP-SG, a unified framework for instance-preserving semantic mapping, functional scene partitioning, and language-guided small, fine-grained object retrieval. OVIP-SG uses a vision-language model (VLM) to enumerate scene-specific categories for robust open-world detection. Symmetric 3D Intersection over Union (IoU) association and area-weighted feature fusion preserve small independent instances, while VLM-inferred object functions partition scenes into compact functional search regions. A four-stage cascaded retrieval pipeline further incorporates voxel voting and determines target absence from exploration coverage. Under a unified evaluation protocol on Replica, OVIP-SG outperforms ConceptGraphs by 6.31 points in class-mean accuracy (mAcc) and 5.15 points in frequency-weighted mIoU (F-mIoU) while achieving a class-agnostic native-instance Panoptic Quality (PQ) of 0.398. It reduces the search area to 21.8% of the indoor floor space and reaches 0.773 balanced accuracy for object-presence classification. Real-world robotic experiments further demonstrate its practical effectiveness.

</details>


### [260] [Collective Ranking of Environmental Signals through Gaussian Belief Propagation in a Patrolling Robot Swarm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17690)
*Zachary R. Madin,Connor York,Jonathan Lawry,Edmund R. Hunt*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出将巡逻图自然对偶地解释为因子图，利用高斯信念传播（GBP）解决多机器人巡逻中的集体排名问题，在仿真和Leo Rovers硬件实验中均优于简单平均和访问次数加权平均方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人巡逻通常以最小化空闲时间为目标，但实际应用（如安全和环境监测）中还需要对巡逻位置进行集体排名（即beyond best-of-n问题的连续值多选项泛化）。现有方法缺乏对空间信念传播的利用，因此需要一种能够同时处理巡逻和排名的新方法。

Method: 利用巡逻图的双重身份——既是决定智能体移动的拓扑结构，也是传播空间信念的因子图。在访问节点上设置单变量测量因子，在巡逻边上设置成对平滑因子，通过高斯信念传播（GBP）这一基于图的算法实现集体排名，并与简单平均、访问次数加权平均进行对比。

Result: 在仿真中，GBP在排名精度、均方误差和达到共识的时间上均优于两种基线方法。随着噪声增加任务变难时，GBP性能优雅退化，而两种平均方法严重退化。硬件实验（四台Leo Rovers在办公楼大厅追踪传播的无线电信号）复现了与仿真相同的性能排序。

Conclusion: GBP框架有效利用巡逻图的因子图结构进行空间信念传播，在集体排名任务中比简单平均方法更稳健、更准确，且仿真结果在真实硬件场景中得到了验证。

Abstract: Multi-robot patrolling requires a team to visit all areas of an environment at regular intervals, typically minimising idleness. A practical extension, motivated by security and environmental monitoring, is to additionally form a collective ranking of all patrol locations by some measured signal, a generalisation of the best-of-n problem to the many-option, continuous-valued regime. We observe that the patrol graph admits a natural dual interpretation: it is simultaneously the topology that dictates agent movement and a factor graph over which spatial beliefs can be propagated. Exploiting this equivalence, we apply Gaussian Belief Propagation (GBP), a graph-based algorithm, to collective ranking using unary measurement factors at visited nodes and pairwise smoothness factors along patrol edges. We compare GBP against simple and visit-count-weighted averaging across a range of sensor-noise conditions in simulation, and validate the approach on four Leo Rovers tracking a propagating radio signal in an office lobby. GBP outperforms both baselines on ranking accuracy, mean squared error, and time to consensus. We find that as noise increases and the task becomes harder, GBP degrades gracefully in simulation while both averaging methods degrade substantially. Hardware trials reproduce the same performance ordering on a real propagating radio signal, supporting the practical relevance of the simulated results.

</details>


### [261] [Force-Based Offset Estimation for Keyed Peg-in-Hole Assembly Using Local Gaussian Process Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17691)
*Chandra Yuvesh Aubeeluck,Abilash Philip Madavath,Augustin Raju,Nicolas Pyschny,Felix Hackelöer,Florian Zwanzig*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于力的偏移估计方法，用于键槽销孔装配（keyed peg-in-hole）任务。该方法嵌入在"感知-验证-插入"流水线中，通过手腕力/力矩传感器数据，利用KNN-高斯过程混合回归器估计残余的径向偏移，并通过区分硬碰撞和导向倒角插入两种接触模式分类路由推理。实验表明，应用该流水线后插入成功率从67%提升至87%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 键槽装配任务具有严格的几何约束，在不确定环境中对抓取姿态偏差高度敏感，传统方法难以准确估计残余偏置，导致插入失败率较高。

Method: 提出"感知-验证-插入"流水线，包含：(1) 利用腕部力/力矩测量，通过局部KNN-高斯过程混合回归器直接估计残余偏置；(2) 通过接触窗口持续时间阈值区分硬碰撞与导向倒角插入两种接触模式，并为每种模式路由到专用模型；(3) 结合抓取后单目视觉验证结果的KNN与确定性搜索，提高回归器精度。

Result: 在协作机器人臂的集成力/力矩传感器实验中，应用该方法后插入成功率从67%提高到87%。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于力和接触模式分类的方法能够有效提高键槽销孔装配在不确定环境下的插入成功率。

Abstract: Key-keyway assembly tasks impose strict geometric constraints and are highly sensitive to grasp pose deviations in uncertain environments. This work presents a force-based offset estimation method for keyed peg-in-hole assembly, embedded within a perception-validation-insertion pipeline. Residual misalignment is estimated directly from wrist force/torque measurements using a local KNN-Gaussian Process hybrid regressor. The framework distinguishes between two contact regimes, hard collision and guided chamfer insertion, and routes inference to a dedicated model for each. Regime classification is achieved via a contact-window duration threshold. KNN combined with a deterministic search using the results of a post-grasp monocular visual validation contributes to an increased accuracy of the regressor model. This approach achieves accurate radial offset estimation in chamfered peg insertion, during a keypoint detection-based pick and place application. Experiments using the integrated force/torque sensor of a collaborative robot arm showed an increase in insertion success rate from 67% to 87% after the pipeline was applied.

</details>


### [262] [Dijkstra as an Oracle for Online Stochastic Shortest Path Navigation with Provable Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17703)
*Mansur M. Arief,Ali Akarma,Ahmad Alfan Alfian Irfan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文证明Dijkstra算法在比文献中常见的因果条件弱得多的条件下仍可作为随机最短路径问题的精确规划引擎，并据此提出在线学习算法DORA用于移动机器人导航，在多个基准任务中相比乐观值迭代减少了4.5至19.3倍规划计算量，同时将动态障碍物接触率控制在预算范围内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动机器人在与人类共存的场景中导航时，地图的真实通行代价通常未知，且执行存在不确定性。精确求解随机最短路径问题（如值迭代）的计算量随地图直径增长，而Dijkstra算法虽然快速，但在随机转移条件下通常被认为是不精确的。作者希望找到一种既快速又精确的规划方案。

Method: 首先在理论上证明Dijkstra算法在"确定性化地图上定义的约化代价非负"这一弱于因果条件的条件下仍可精确求解随机最短路径问题。基于此刻画，提出DORA（Dijkstra Oracle Reduced-cost Algorithm）在线学习算法：每回合固定次数调用最短路径预言机，不需要估计转移核，并在动态障碍物接触概率需保持在预算内时加入对数存活权重。

Result: 在网格世界导航、定向钻井和无人机监视三个基准实验中，DORA与使用真实转移核的乐观值迭代效果相当，但规划工作量减少4.5至19.3倍；相比确定化+重规划方法，学习过程中的接触次数减少17倍；接触率保持在跨越两个数量级的预算范围内。

Conclusion: 最短路径搜索可以支撑安全且高效的在线导航和路径规划任务，Dijkstra算法在适当条件下能够作为精确且快速的随机规划引擎。

Abstract: Mobile robots that operate in side by side with humans and critical facilities must reach their goals at low cost, despite often unknown true traversal costs of the map apriori and imperfect actuation. Planners that solve the underlying stochastic shortest path problem exactly, such as value iteration, require computation that grows with the diameter of the map, whereas Dijkstra's algorithm is fast but is usually considered inexact once transitions are stochastic. This study shows that Dijkstra's algorithm can remain an exact planning engine under a condition that is much weaker than the causality condition often invoked in the literature, namely nonnegativity of a reduced cost defined on the determinized map. Building on this characterization, an online learner DORA (Dijkstra Oracle Reduced-cost Algorithm) is proposed for robot navigation that calls a shortest path oracle a fixed number of times per episode, never estimates a transition kernel, and adds a logarithmic survival weight when the probability of contact with a dynamic obstacle must stay within a budget. In the numerical experiments involving three other benchmarks that cover grid world navigation, directional drilling, and drone surveillance, the learner matches optimistic value iteration that is given the true transition kernel while performing 4.5 to 19.3 times less planner work, reduces contacts during learning by a factor of seventeen relative to determinize and replan, and keeps the contact rate within budgets that span two orders of magnitude. These results indicate that shortest path search supports safe and efficient online navigation and path planning tasks.

</details>


### [263] [CompCPZ: Preserving Multi-Modal Intent in Language-Guided Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17717)
*Zhen Zhang,Ahmad Hafez,Peng Xie,Yanliang Huang,Wenyuan Wu,Amr Alanwar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出CompCPZ，一个基于约束多项式Zonotope的代数层，可叠加在语言条件学习系统之上，恢复析取指令的多模态表示，在仿真和真实机器人上均显著优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的语言条件机器人策略存在结构性缺陷：将析取指令（如"放在红盘子或蓝盘子附近"）折叠成单一连通集合，导致表示无法保留所有可行模态，且规划器在运行时的模态不确定性下性能退化。

Method: CompCPZ是一个声音代数层，沿着语言解析树递归组合每个原语的约束多项式Zonotope包围，并使用无分布共形覆盖，实现亚毫秒级运行。

Result: 在ManiSkill3桌面操作基准上，CompCPZ以1,900/1,918的配对胜率（p << 10^-30）击败凸集基线、多峰解码器和零样本视觉语言动作模型；该编译器无需重新调参即可迁移到Unitree Go2四足机器人的真实平面实验。

Conclusion: 组合语言接地不仅应评估是否到达解码目标，更应评估所表示的可行集是否保留了用户意图的连通分量结构。

Abstract: A robot asked to "place the cup near the red plate or the blue plate" may reach the centroid between them and appear geometrically successful, while satisfying neither disjunct of the instruction. This silent semantic failure exposes a structural limitation of language-conditioned robot policies: representations that collapse a disjunctive instruction into a single connected set cannot preserve all feasible modes, and planners that commit to one action degrade under run-time mode uncertainty. We address this limitation with CompCPZ, a sound algebraic layer that language-conditioned learning systems wrap to recover multi-modal disjunctive representation, recursively composing per-primitive constrained polynomial zonotope enclosures along the language parse tree with distribution-free conformal coverage and sub-millisecond runtime. On a closed-loop ManiSkill3 tabletop-manipulation benchmark, CompCPZ outperforms convex set baselines, multi-peak decoders, and a zero-shot vision-language-action model (1,900/1,918 paired wins, p << 10^(-30)); the same compiler also transfers without retuning to planar real-robot trials on a Unitree Go2 quadruped under motion capture. These results suggest that compositional language grounding should be evaluated not only by reaching a decoded target, but by whether the represented feasibility set preserves the connected-component structure of the user's intent.

</details>


### [264] [Stability Control for Real World Testing in Autonomous Racing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17779)
*Phillip Pitschi,Simon Sagmeister,Frederik Werner,Markus Lienkamp,Boris Lohmann*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套全面的车辆稳定性控制系统，由电子稳定控制（ESC）、滑移控制（SC）和反向转向系统（CS）组成，用于保障自动驾驶车辆在动力学极限下的安全运行。该系统通过调整运动控制器的转向和制动指令来维持车辆稳定性，并在仿真和全尺寸实车实验中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶车辆在极限操控条件下（如自动驾驶赛车）容易因道路状况或天气等外部因素而失稳，而大多数控制算法假设车辆处于稳定状态，在临界情况下可能失效。尤其是无安全驾驶员乘坐的昂贵车辆，安全问题尤为突出，因此需要一套综合的稳定性控制系统来保障安全运行。

Method: 提出了一个三层稳定性控制系统框架，包括：1）电子稳定控制（ESC）——调整制动指令以纠正车辆横摆；2）滑移控制（SC）——控制轮胎滑移率防止打滑；3）反向转向系统（CS）——在必要时主动调整转向角度以恢复稳定性。三者协同工作，自适应地修正运动控制器输出的转向和制动指令。

Result: 通过仿真和全尺寸实车实验验证，该稳定性控制系统在临界情况下能够有效维持车辆稳定性，并扩展了车辆的可操作可行区域。系统代码已在GitHub上开源，便于集成使用。

Conclusion: 该稳定性控制系统能够显著提升自动驾驶车辆在动力学极限下的安全性和操控性，为自动驾驶赛车等高风险场景提供了可靠的安全保障机制，同时开源实现降低了工程集成的门槛。

Abstract: Controlling an autonomous vehicle at the limits of handling is a challenging task. Due to external influences, such as road conditions or weather, a vehicle can easily become unstable. Since most control algorithms assume stable vehicle behavior, they might fail in these situations. Especially when operating expensive vehicles without a safety driver on board, as in autonomous racing, this poses a significant challenge. To enable safe operation at the vehicle's dynamic limits, we present a comprehensive stability control system that safeguards motion control algorithms in autonomous driving. The proposed system consists of an electronic stability control (ESC), a slip control (SC), and a countersteer system (CS), which collectively adapt steering and brake commands from the motion controller to maintain vehicle stability. We validate our approach through both simulation and experiments on a real-world, full-scale vehicle. The results show that the stability control system maintains vehicle stability in critical situations and extends the operational feasible region. To simplify integration, we provide an open-source implementation at github.com/TUMFTM/tam-stability-control.

</details>


### [265] [Effector-Centric NMPC of Tiltable-Multirotors for Offset-Free Omnidirectional Aerial Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17819)
*Jinjie Li,Yicheng Chen,Johannes Kübel,Haokun Liu,Junichiro Sugihara,Moju Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对可倾转多旋翼无人机在航空操作中的应用，提出了设计分析与基于力/力矩的控制框架。通过四旋翼可倾转配置平衡螺旋桨尺寸与悬停效率，利用零空间冗余穿越奇异构型，并采用改进积分项与加速度估计器进行扰动补偿，最终实现了100 Hz板载运行的NMPC框架，并通过90度翻滚、白板推压和360度阀门转动等真实实验验证了可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统带机械臂的航空系统操作范围有限且效率不高，而可倾转多旋翼能通过飞行底座直接产生六自由度力/力矩，实现高效移动与全方位操作。但该类系统在设计选择、奇异构型穿越和扰动补偿方面仍缺乏系统性分析与统一控制框架。

Method: 提出一种基于力/力矩的可倾转多旋翼控制框架：1) 对四旋翼可倾转配置进行设计分析，论证其螺旋桨尺寸与悬停效率间的平衡及零空间冗余的作用；2) 采用向上端执行器布局以权衡几何间隙与可用力/力矩；3) 提出改进积分项（处理模型误差）与基于加速度的外部力/力矩估计器相结合的扰动补偿策略；4) 整合以上因素构建端执行器中心的非线性模型预测控制（NMPC）统一框架。

Result: 该框架在自建可倾转四旋翼上以100 Hz频率完全板载运行。真实世界实验包括90度阶跃翻滚、白板推压和连续360度阀门转动，证明了在单自由度每臂可倾转四旋翼上实现基于力/力矩的全方位操作与奇异穿越的可行性。

Conclusion: 研究表明四旋翼可倾转配置是航空操作中兼具效率与操作能力的设计选择，向上端执行器布局与统一的NMPC框架可有效实现带奇异穿越的全方位操作，为实际可倾转多旋翼的航空操纵提供了可行的系统化解决方案。

Abstract: Aerial manipulation extends robotic operations to previously inaccessible aerial environments. Unlike arm-equipped aerial systems, tiltable-multirotors can directly generate six-degree-of-freedom wrenches through their flight bases, enabling both efficient movement and omnidirectional operation by tilting the thrust direction.
  This work presents a design analysis and a wrench-based control framework for tiltable-multirotors in aerial manipulation. We show that a four-rotor tiltable configuration provides a balance between interference-free propeller sizing and hovering efficiency across different attitudes, and its null-space redundancy is crucial for traversing singular configurations under physical constraints. We further show that an upward end-effector placement yields a favorable trade-off between geometric clearance and available wrench. To address disturbances, we propose a dual strategy consisting of a modified integral term for model error and an acceleration-based estimator for external wrenches. Building on these insights, we develop an effector-centric nonlinear model predictive control (NMPC) framework that integrates design choices, singularity handling, and disturbance compensation into a unified formulation.
  The proposed framework runs fully onboard at 100 Hz on a custom-built tiltable-quadrotor. Real-world experiments, including a 90-deg step cartwheel rotation, whiteboard pushing, and continuous 360-deg valve turning, demonstrate the feasibility of wrench-based omnidirectional manipulation with singularity traversal on a one-DoF-per-arm tiltable-quadrotor.

</details>


### [266] [Jetson-ORB-SLAM3: Accuracy-Preserving GPU Implementation for Edge Computing Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17874)
*Rajat Roy,Aditya Arun Kumar Yadav,Hardik Jain*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种保精度的ORB-SLAM3 GPU移植方案，针对NVIDIA Jetson Orin Nano低功耗边缘平台。GPU ORB前端与CPU参考实现算法一致，关键点一致率达94.7%，描述子位一致率99.9%。回环检测通过原生TensorRT的FP16引擎实现180倍加速，每查询仅2.2ms。在EuRoC、TUM-VI和KITTI数据集上验证了GPU移植与硬件更换均不改变估计轨迹，单目惯性模式在11个EuRoC序列上平均达到32FPS，功耗仅7W。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低功耗边缘平台上的视觉惯性SLAM受限于稠密特征提取和回环检测的计算成本。现有的GPU加速ORB-SLAM通过近似ORB检测器换取速度，从而改变了特征集和估计轨迹，牺牲了精度。作者希望在不损失精度的情况下实现边缘平台上的GPU加速SLAM。

Method: 将视觉前端（特征提取）卸载到GPU，而将建图和优化后端保留在CPU上，使每个计算匹配其所适合的硬件。GPU ORB前端在算法层面复现参考CPU检测器。回环检测采用CosPlace ResNet-50，通过原生libnvinfer FP16引擎实现边缘平台上的可行推理。使用四种配置（GPU流水线和未修改的CPU参考在Jetson Orin Nano和桌面上各运行一次）进行精度验证。

Result: 在EuRoC数据集上，四种配置的平均绝对轨迹误差(SE(3))差异在0.10cm以内，证明GPU移植和硬件更换均不改变估计轨迹。GPU与CPU对比在TUM-VI和KITTI数据集上可复现。在EuRoC上与已发表的ORB-SLAM3竞争，TUM-VI六个房间序列中五个达到亚厘米精度，KITTI十一个序列中九个达到低于1%的相对平移误差。回环查询从不可用加速到2.2ms（180倍加速），单目惯性模式在11个EuRoC序列上平均32FPS，功耗7W。

Conclusion: 该GPU实现是保精度的而非近似加速，在NVIDIA Jetson Orin Nano上实现了与CPU参考一致的轨迹精度。原生TensorRT FP16引擎使基于CNN的闭环检测在7W低功耗设备上可与跟踪并发运行，为低功耗边缘平台的实时SLAM提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Visual-inertial SLAM on low-power edge platforms is constrained by the cost of dense feature extraction and loop closure. Prior GPU ports of ORB-SLAM trade accuracy for speed by approximating the ORB detector, altering the feature set and therefore the estimated trajectory. We present an accuracy-preserving GPU implementation of ORB-SLAM3 for the NVIDIA Jetson Orin Nano, whose GPU ORB front end reproduces the reference CPU detector algorithmically to 94.7% exact keypoint agreement and 99.9% descriptor bit agreement. This work also makes CNN-based loop closure edge-viable through native TensorRT. The visual front end (feature extraction) is offloaded to the GPU while the mapping and optimization back end is kept on the CPU, matching each computation to the hardware it suits. The accuracy is verified by comparing four configurations: the GPU pipeline and the unmodified CPU reference, each run on both the Jetson Orin Nano and a desktop. On EuRoC dataset, all four agree to within 0.10cm in mean absolute trajectory error (SE(3)), so neither the GPU port nor the change of hardware shifts the estimated trajectory. The GPU-versus-CPU comparison is reproducible on TUM-VI and KITTI datasets, so the acceleration is accuracy-preserving rather than approximate. The proposed implementation is competitive with published ORB-SLAM3 on EuRoC, attains sub-centimeter accuracy on five of the six TUM-VI room sequences, and reaches sub-1% relative translation error on nine of eleven KITTI sequences. For loop closure, the generic ONNX-Runtime CUDA/TensorRT execution providers are unusable with our CosPlace ResNet-50 on the embedded platform, whereas a native libnvinfer FP16 engine reduces per-query inference to 2.2ms, a 180x speedup. Learned place recognition therefore runs concurrently with tracking on a 7W device. In monocular-inertial mode the system sustains 32FPS mean over the eleven EuRoC sequences.

</details>


### [267] [ControlledShifts: Towards Standardizing Robustness Evaluation in Trajectory Prediction Under Distribution Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17882)
*Ingrid navarro,Pablo Ortega-Kral,Yutong Duan,Jonathan Francis,Jean Oh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出ControlledShifts框架和基准套件，通过系统的数据重划分来评估轨迹预测模型在分布偏移下的鲁棒性，并提出了统一的鲁棒性评分方法，最终对多种transformer架构进行了基准测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的轨迹预测方法在训练数据覆盖不足的场景下性能会急剧下降，但各种缓解分布偏移的方法缺乏统一的评估标准，无法在模型可能遇到的多种偏移轴上进行系统化比较，导致该领域缺乏标准化的鲁棒性对比方式。

Method: 提出ControlledShifts框架，通过共享的"特征化-分割"公式系统地将轨迹数据集重分为分布内和分布外两部分；构建三个针对关键拓扑和行为分布偏移的基准；提出统一的鲁棒性评分，从预测质量（相对性能增益）和预测稳定性（偏移下性能保持）两个维度进行评估。

Result: 通过对多种transformer架构进行基准测试，揭示了不同规模模型在处理潜在相关性和环境结构方面的关键差异，展示了不同模型容量在分布偏移下的鲁棒性表现差异。

Conclusion: ControlledShifts为轨迹预测模型的分布偏移鲁棒性提供了标准化的评估框架，填补了该领域缺乏统一鲁棒性比较标准的空白。

Abstract: Trajectory prediction is central to safety in autonomous driving, yet learning-based predictors tend to degrade sharply when encountering scenarios poorly represented by their training data. Many methods attempt to mitigate distribution shift degradation through data-centric or test-time adaptation approaches; however, they are typically validated along fragmented axes of generalization, leaving the field without a standardized way to compare robustness across shifts a model may encounter.
  To address this, we introduce ControlledShifts, a framework and benchmark suite that systematically re-splits existing trajectory datasets into in-distribution (seen) and out-of-distribution (unseen) partitions, via a shared characterization-and-splitting formulation, in which a characterization function fixes the axis of variation a benchmark probes and a splitting function fixes how the tail of that axis is withheld. The suite comprises three benchmarks targeting key topological and behavioral distribution shifts. Furthermore, to aggregate multi-dimensional performance metrics across these benchmarks, we propose a unified robustness score that evaluates models along two complementary dimensions: prediction quality (relative performance gain) and prediction stability (performance preservation under shift). We showcase ControlledShifts by benchmarking prominent transformer-based architectures, exposing critical differences in how models of varying capacities handle latent relevance and environmental structure.

</details>


### [268] [PRISM: Precision and contact-rich Real-world Industrial Skill dataset with Multimodal sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17962)
*Tengbo Yu,Jiahao Wu,Hanning Wang,Rui Chen,Chuanhou Liu,Chuang Sun,Hangxin Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PRISM是一个大规模多模态数据集，专门面向接触密集的工业操作任务，包含25余种操作任务、5000余条轨迹、共45小时的遥操作演示，并同步采集多视角RGB-D、力/力矩、触觉和机器人状态数据，为高精度工业环境下的多模态感知与控制提供了现实基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人学习数据集大多集中于如抓取放置等短时程、低接触任务，缺乏对工业装配所需的精确控制、力/力矩或触觉调节及多模态反馈的覆盖，因此需要构建一个面向接触密集工业操作的大规模数据集。

Method: 通过遥操作方式采集超过25种工业操作任务（如电子元件插拔、传送带分拣等）的演示数据，总计5000余条轨迹、45小时，并同步记录多视角RGB-D、力/力矩、触觉和机器人状态等多模态数据。

Result: PRISM提供了覆盖多样化机械约束的大规模多模态数据集，与家庭或实验室场景相比，在高精度工业约束下为多模态感知与控制提供了更真实的基准，可作为接触密集、可泛化操作学习的基石。

Conclusion: PRISM填补了工业接触密集操作数据集的空白，为真实制造环境中多模态感知和高精度力控操作研究提供了开放的基础设施，数据集已开源发布。

Abstract: Recent progress in robotic learning has been fueled by large-scale datasets collected in everyday environments. However, most existing datasets emphasize short-horizon, low-contact tasks such as pick-and-place, and therefore do not capture the precision control, force/torque or tactile regulation, and multimodal feedback required for industrial assembly. To address this gap, we introduce PRISM, a large-scale multimodal dataset for contact-rich industrial operations. The dataset spans more than 25 manipulation tasks (e.g., electronic components plug/unplug, conveyor-based sorting) and covers diverse mechanical constraints. PRISM includes more than 5,000 trajectories totaling 45 hours of teleoperated demonstrations, recorded using synchronized multi-view RGB-D, force/torque, tactile, and robot-state measurements. In contrast to datasets collected in household or laboratory settings, PRISM provides a realistic benchmark for multimodal perception and control under high-precision industrial constraints, and serves as a foundation for contact-rich, generalizable manipulation in real-world manufacturing environments. The dataset is open-sourced at: https://tengbo-yu.github.io/PRISM/

</details>


### [269] [Hydra-0: Action Flow for Generalist World Modeling and Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18077)
*Hongyu Li,Bowen Wen,Xinghao Zhu,Yixuan Wang,Yilun Du,Yunzhu Li,George Konidaris,Stan Birchfield,Soha Pouya,Chenran Li,Yan Chang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Hydra-0，一种以动作流（action flow）为条件的世界模型，将机器人动作表示为像素运动，实现跨本体、任务、环境和视频生成骨干的通用世界建模与控制。最佳配置相比基线降低了90.4%的机器人运动误差和60.2%的对象运动误差，支持零样本组合和数据高效适配，在RoboLab基准上重放与参考成功率的Pearson相关系数达0.96。此外还发现该接口的逆模式——世界动作模型，可从人类演示的期望对象流预测兼容的机器人运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人世界模型通常受限于特定本体、任务和环境，难以利用异构的训练数据和视频生成骨干进行泛化。需要一个共享的视觉接口来表示机器人动作，从而统一不同数据源和跨模态的建模与控制。

Method: 将机器人动作表示为像素运动（动作流），作为条件化世界模型的共享视觉接口。通过预测动作流驱动的后续帧来实现世界建模，并利用一个动作头将潜在特征映射为可执行动作。该方法支持跨本体、跨任务、跨环境的泛化，并可迁移到不同的视频生成骨干。

Result: 最佳配置相比动作条件化基线降低了90.4%的机器人运动误差和60.2%的对象运动误差；支持零样本组合与数据高效适配；在RoboLab基准上重放与参考成功率的Pearson相关系数r=0.96，展示了开放回路策略评估的有效性；逆模式（世界动作模型）可从人类演示的对象流预测兼容的机器人动作。

Conclusion: 动作流作为一种共享控制接口，能够连接异构训练数据、开放回路策略评估和机器人控制，展示了通用世界模型在机器人学习和控制中的潜力。

Abstract: We introduce Hydra-0, a generalist world model conditioned on action flow, which represents robot actions as pixel motion. This shared visual interface enables generalist world modeling and control by learning action consequences across embodiments, tasks, environments, and video-generation backbones. Our best configuration achieves 90.4% lower robot-motion error and 60.2% lower object-motion error than our action-conditioned baseline, while supporting zero-shot composition and data-efficient adaptation. On the RoboLab benchmark, Hydra-0 achieves a Pearson correlation of r=0.96 between replayed and reference success rates. Finally, we uncover an emergent inverse mode of this interface: a world action model that predicts compatible robot motion from desired object flow transferred from a human demonstration. A trained action head maps the resulting latent features to executable actions without requiring task-specific expert robot demonstrations. Together, these results demonstrate the potential of action flow as a shared control interface connecting heterogeneous training data, open-loop policy evaluation, and robot control.
